            ).show()
        }

        btnClearAllMulti?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            config.multiTemplateIndices.clear()
            refreshMultiTemplatesGrid()
            Toast.makeText(this, "❌ Все шаблоны убраны из мультипоиска", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        var selectedType = config.type

        fun updateUI() {
            val isClick = selectedType == ActionType.CLICK
            val isHold = selectedType == ActionType.LONG_PRESS
            val isSwipe = selectedType == ActionType.SWIPE || selectedType == ActionType.PATH
            val isTrigger = selectedType == ActionType.TRIGGER

            btnTypeClick?.backgroundTintList =
                ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(if (isClick) R.color.accent_blue else R.color.panel_blue))
            btnTypeClick?.setTextColor(if (isClick) Color.WHITE else Color.parseColor("#8B949E"))

            btnTypeHold?.backgroundTintList =
                ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(if (isHold) R.color.accent_blue else R.color.panel_blue))
            btnTypeHold?.setTextColor(if (isHold) Color.WHITE else Color.parseColor("#8B949E"))

            btnTypeSwipe?.backgroundTintList =
                ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(if (isSwipe) R.color.accent_blue else R.color.panel_blue))
            btnTypeSwipe?.setTextColor(if (isSwipe) Color.WHITE else Color.parseColor("#8B949E"))

            btnTypeTrigger?.backgroundTintList =
                ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(if (isTrigger) R.color.accent_blue else R.color.panel_blue))
            btnTypeTrigger?.setTextColor(if (isTrigger) Color.WHITE else Color.parseColor("#8B949E"))

            tvHoldTitle?.visibility = if (isHold) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            etHoldDuration?.visibility = if (isHold) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            val layoutAiBlock = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(dialogView, "layoutAiParametersBlock")
            layoutAiBlock?.visibility = if (isTrigger) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            val tvAiTimeoutTitle = findViewByName<TextView>(dialogView, "tvAiTimeoutTitle")
            val etAiTimeout = findViewByName<EditText>(dialogView, "etAiTimeout")
            tvAiTimeoutTitle?.visibility = if (isTrigger) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            etAiTimeout?.visibility = if (isTrigger) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            etAiTimeout?.setText(config.aiTimeoutSeconds.toString())
        }
        updateUI()

        btnTypeClick?.setOnClickListener { selectedType = ActionType.CLICK; updateUI() }
        btnTypeHold?.setOnClickListener { selectedType = ActionType.LONG_PRESS; updateUI() }
        btnTypeSwipe?.setOnClickListener { selectedType = ActionType.SWIPE; updateUI() }
        btnTypeTrigger?.setOnClickListener { selectedType = ActionType.TRIGGER; updateUI() }

        fun saveCurrentStepData() {
            config.type = selectedType
            val delaySec = etDelay?.text?.toString()?.toDoubleOrNull() ?: 1.0
            config.delay = (delaySec * 1000).toLong().coerceAtLeast(50L)
            config.repeatCount =
                etRepeatCount?.text?.toString()?.toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1) ?: 1

            val etAiTimeout = findViewByName<EditText>(dialogView, "etAiTimeout")
            config.aiTimeoutSeconds =
                etAiTimeout?.text?.toString()?.toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(0) ?: 2

            if (selectedType == ActionType.SWIPE && config.endView == null) {
                val loc = IntArray(2)
                config.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
                spawnEndTargetAtPosition(
                    config,
                    loc[0].toFloat() + dpToPx(80),
                    loc[1].toFloat() + dpToPx(80)
                )
            } else if (selectedType != ActionType.SWIPE && config.endView != null) {
                safeRemoveView(config.endView)
                config.endView = null
            }
        }

        val btnCalibrateMatchesOnScreen =
            findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnCalibrateMatchesOnScreen")
        btnCalibrateMatchesOnScreen?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            saveCurrentStepData()
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            startTemplateCalibration(config)
        }

        val btnSearchAreaConfig = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnTemplateSearchArea")
        btnSearchAreaConfig?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            showCustomSearchAreaSelector(config) { customRect ->
                if (config.selectedTemplateIndex in globalTemplatesNames.indices) {
                    val tPath = globalTemplatesNames[config.selectedTemplateIndex]
                    saveTemplateMetadata(
                        tPath,
                        config.similarityPercent,
                        customArea = customRect != null,
                        sX = customRect?.left ?: 0,
                        sY = customRect?.top ?: 0,
                        sW = customRect?.width() ?: 0,
                        sH = customRect?.height() ?: 0
                    )
                }
                showEditDialog(config)
            }
        }

        btnCloneAction?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            saveCurrentStepData()
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)

            val loc = IntArray(2)
            config.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
            addNewActionAtPosition(
                loc[0].toFloat() + dpToPx(20),
                loc[1].toFloat() + dpToPx(20),
                config.delay,
                config.type,
                config.selectedTemplateIndex
            )
            Toast.makeText(this, "📋 Шаг #${config.id} успешно клонирован!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT)
                .show()
        }

        btnDeleteAction?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            safeRemoveView(config.startView)
            config.endView?.let { safeRemoveView(it) }
            actionsList.remove(config)

            for (i in actionsList.indices) {
                val act = actionsList[i]
                act.id = i + 1
                act.startView.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumber)?.text = act.id.toString()
                act.endView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumberEnd)?.text = "${act.id}E"
            }
            Toast.makeText(this, "🗑 Шаг удален", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        btnPrevStep?.setOnClickListener {
            saveCurrentStepData()
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            if (currentStepIdx > 0) showEditDialog(actionsList[currentStepIdx - 1])
        }

        btnNextStep?.setOnClickListener {
            saveCurrentStepData()
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            if (currentStepIdx < actionsList.size - 1) showEditDialog(actionsList[currentStepIdx + 1])
        }

        val performSave = {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            saveCurrentStepData()
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
            Toast.makeText(this, "Параметры шага #${config.id} сохранены!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT)
                .show()
        }

        val performClose = {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
        }

        btnSaveHeader?.setOnClickListener { performSave() }
        btnCloseHeader?.setOnClickListener { performClose() }
        btnSave?.setOnClickListener { performSave() }
        btnCancel?.setOnClickListener { performClose() }

        safeAddView(dialogView, params)
    }

    fun showScriptsDialog() {
        val dialogView = LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.dialog_scripts, null)
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_DIM_BEHIND or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            dimAmount = 0.5f
        }

        val etScriptName = findViewByName<EditText>(dialogView, "etScriptName")
        val btnSaveScriptAction = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnSaveScriptAction")
        val layoutScriptsList = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(dialogView, "layoutScriptsList")
        val btnCloseScripts = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnCloseScripts")
        val btnCloseScriptsHeader = findViewByName<ImageButton>(dialogView, "btnCloseScriptsHeader")
        btnCloseScriptsHeader?.setOnClickListener { safeRemoveView(dialogView) }

        fun refreshScriptsList() {
            layoutScriptsList?.removeAllViews()
            val dir = File(filesDir, "scripts")
            if (dir.exists()) {
                dir.listFiles()?.forEach { file ->
                    if (file.name.endsWith(".json")) {
                        val itemView = LayoutInflater.from(this@MyAutoClickService)
                            .inflate(R.layout.item_script, null)
                        val tvName = itemView.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvScriptName)
                        val btnExport = itemView.findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnExportScriptFile)
                        val btnCopy = itemView.findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnCopyScriptFile)
                        val btnDelete = itemView.findViewById<Button>(R.id.btnDeleteScriptFile)

                        tvName.text = file.nameWithoutExtension
                        tvName.setOnClickListener {
                            loadScriptByName(file.nameWithoutExtension)
                            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
                        }

                        btnExport?.setOnClickListener {
                            exportScriptWithTemplates(
                                this@MyAutoClickService,
                                file.nameWithoutExtension
                            )
                        }

                        btnCopy?.setOnClickListener {
                            try {
                                val copyName = "${file.nameWithoutExtension}_копия_${System.currentTimeMillis() % 1000}"
                                val copyFile = File(dir, "$copyName.json")
                                file.copyTo(copyFile, overwrite = true)
                                refreshScriptsList()
                                Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "📋 Сценарий скопирован!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            } catch (e: Exception) {
                                logError(this@MyAutoClickService, e)
                            }
                        }

                        btnDelete?.setOnClickListener {
                            file.delete()
                            refreshScriptsList()
                        }
                        layoutScriptsList?.addView(itemView)
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        refreshScriptsList()

        btnSaveScriptAction?.setOnClickListener {
            val name = etScriptName?.text?.toString()?.trim() ?: ""
            if (name.isNotEmpty()) {
                saveScriptByName(name)
                refreshScriptsList()
                etScriptName?.setText("")
            }
        }

        btnCloseScripts?.setOnClickListener { safeRemoveView(dialogView) }
        safeAddView(dialogView, params)
    }

    private fun addNewActionAtPosition(
        posX: Float,
        posY: Float,
        recordedDelay: Long,
        actionType: ActionType,
        templateIndex: Int
    ) {
        val actionId = actionsList.size + 1
        val startView = LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.floating_target, null)
        val tvNum = startView.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumber)
        tvNum.text = actionId.toString()
        tvNum.visibility = if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE

        val initialSizeDp = if (actionType == ActionType.TRIGGER) 50 else 36
        val sizePx = dpToPx(initialSizeDp)

        val touchFlags = if (isRecording || isPlaying) {
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS
        } else {
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS
        }

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            sizePx,
            sizePx,
            getOverlayType(),
            touchFlags,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = (posX - sizePx / 2f).toInt()
            y = (posY - sizePx / 2f).toInt()
        }

        val config = ActionConfig(
            id = actionId,
            startView = startView,
            type = actionType,
            delay = recordedDelay,
            selectedTemplateIndex = templateIndex,
            repeatCount = 0
        )
        if (actionType == ActionType.TRIGGER) {
            config.multiTemplateIndices = ArrayList(globalTemplates.indices.toList())
        }

        startView.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initialX = 0
            private var initialY = 0
            private var initialTouchX = 0f
            private var initialTouchY = 0f
            private var isMoving = false

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (isPlaying) return false

                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initialX = params.x
                        initialY = params.y
                        initialTouchX = event.rawX
                        initialTouchY = event.rawY
                        isMoving = false
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val dx = abs(event.rawX - initialTouchX)
                        val dy = abs(event.rawY - initialTouchY)
                        if (dx > 8 || dy > 8) {
                            isMoving = true
                            val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
                            val startSize = if (startView.width > 0) startView.width else dpToPx(36)
                            params.x = (initialX + (event.rawX - initialTouchX).toInt()).coerceIn(
                                0,
                                displayMetrics.widthPixels - startSize
                            )
                            params.y = (initialY + (event.rawY - initialTouchY).toInt()).coerceIn(
                                0,
                                displayMetrics.heightPixels - startSize
                            )
                            safeUpdateViewLayout(startView, params)
                        }
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        v.performClick()
                        if (!isMoving && !isPlaying) {
                            vibrateFeedback(25L)
                            showTargetQuickRing(config)
                        }
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        if (isRecording) {
            startView.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        }

        actionsList.add(config)
        safeAddView(startView, params)
    }

    private fun showTargetQuickRing(config: ActionConfig) {
        activeQuickRingView?.let { safeRemoveView(it); activeQuickRingView = null }

        val loc = IntArray(2)
        config.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
        val size = if (config.startView.width > 0) config.startView.width else dpToPx(36)
        val centerX = loc[0] + size / 2f
        val centerY = loc[1] + size / 2f

        val ringView = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#FA0D1117"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(16).toFloat()
                setStroke(dpToPx(1.5f), Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
            }
            val pad = dpToPx(4)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
            elevation = dpToPx(12).toFloat()
        }

        val btnEdit = Button(this).apply {
            text = "⚙️"
            textSize = 12f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                activeQuickRingView?.let { safeRemoveView(it); activeQuickRingView = null }
                showEditDialog(config)
            }
        }
        ringView.addView(btnEdit, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dpToPx(32), dpToPx(32)).apply { marginEnd = dpToPx(4) })

        val btnTest = Button(this).apply {
            text = "▶"
            textSize = 12f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(25L)
                showClickVisualizer(centerX, centerY)
                performClick(centerX, centerY, globalClickDurationMs)
            }
        }
        ringView.addView(btnTest, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dpToPx(32), dpToPx(32)).apply { marginEnd = dpToPx(4) })

        val btnClone = Button(this).apply {
            text = "📋"
            textSize = 12f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(25L)
                activeQuickRingView?.let { safeRemoveView(it); activeQuickRingView = null }
                addNewActionAtPosition(centerX + dpToPx(20), centerY + dpToPx(20), config.delay, config.type, config.selectedTemplateIndex)
                Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "📋 Шаг #${config.id} клонирован!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        ringView.addView(btnClone, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dpToPx(32), dpToPx(32)).apply { marginEnd = dpToPx(4) })

        val btnDelete = Button(this).apply {
            text = "🗑"
            textSize = 12f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(30L)
                activeQuickRingView?.let { safeRemoveView(it); activeQuickRingView = null }
                safeRemoveView(config.startView)
                config.endView?.let { ev -> safeRemoveView(ev) }
                actionsList.remove(config)
                Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "🗑 Шаг удален", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        ringView.addView(btnDelete, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dpToPx(32), dpToPx(32)))

        val ringW = dpToPx(150)
        val ringH = dpToPx(40)
        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
        val ringX = (centerX - ringW / 2f).toInt().coerceIn(dpToPx(8), displayMetrics.widthPixels - ringW - dpToPx(8))
        val ringY = (centerY - ringH - dpToPx(24)).toInt().coerceIn(dpToPx(40), displayMetrics.heightPixels - ringH - dpToPx(20))

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            ringW, ringH,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = ringX; y = ringY
        }

        activeQuickRingView = ringView
        safeAddView(ringView, params)
        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
            if (activeQuickRingView == ringView) {
                safeRemoveView(ringView)
                activeQuickRingView = null
            }
        }, 4000L)
    }

    private fun showMethodHelpDialog() {
        val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
        val helpCard = LinearLayout(contextThemeWrapper).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#FA0D1117"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(16).toFloat()
                setStroke(dpToPx(1.5f), Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
            }
            val p = dpToPx(14)
            setPadding(p, p, p, p)
            elevation = dpToPx(20).toFloat()
        }

        val tvTitle = TextView(this).apply {
            text = "💡 Как выбрать метод поиска?"
            setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
            textSize = 14f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, dpToPx(8))
        }
        helpCard.addView(tvTitle)

        val tvBody = TextView(this).apply {
            text = """1. ⚡ Контурный / Строгий:
• Для четких кнопок, интерфейсов и меню.
• Ищет точный контур с учетом контраста.

2. 🔥 Анимационный (Огонь / Динамика):
• Для объектов с анимацией (пламя, вспышки, эффекты).
• Сглаживает микро-сдвиги пикселей и расширяет допуск по цвету.

3. 🔘 Круг / 🔲 Прямоугольник:
• Круг отсекает угловой фоновый шум у круглой иконки.
• Прямоугольник подходит под квадратные плашки."""
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 11f
            setLineSpacing(dpToPx(2).toFloat(), 1.1f)
        }
        helpCard.addView(tvBody)

        val btnCloseHelp = Button(this).apply {
            text = "ПОНЯТНО ✔"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 11f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#58A6FF"))
        }
        helpCard.addView(btnCloseHelp, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(38)).apply {
            topMargin = dpToPx(10)
        })

        val helpParams = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            dpToPx(300),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        }

        btnCloseHelp.setOnClickListener {
            safeRemoveView(helpCard)
        }

        safeAddView(helpCard, helpParams)
    }

    private var wakeLock: android.os.PowerManager.WakeLock? = null
    private var screenLockOverlayView: View? = null
    private var activeQuickRingView: View? = null

    private fun acquireWakeLock() {
        try {
            if (wakeLock == null) {
                val pm = getSystemService(POWER_SERVICE) as? android.os.PowerManager
                wakeLock = pm?.newWakeLock(android.os.PowerManager.PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK, "AutoTap::ExecutionWakeLock")
            }
            if (wakeLock?.isHeld == false) {
                wakeLock?.acquire(60 * 60 * 1000L)
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private fun releaseWakeLock() {
        try {
            if (wakeLock?.isHeld == true) {
                wakeLock?.release()
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    private var searchAreaOverlayView: View? = null

    fun showCustomSearchAreaSelector(targetConfig: ActionConfig?, onAreaSelected: (Rect?) -> Unit) {
        if (searchAreaOverlayView != null) return
        val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
        val frameView = LayoutInflater.from(contextThemeWrapper).inflate(R.layout.floating_search_area_frame, null)
        
        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
        val screenW = displayMetrics.widthPixels
        val screenH = displayMetrics.heightPixels

        val viewFrame = findViewByName<View>(frameView, "viewSearchAreaFrame")
        val handleMove = findViewByName<TextView>(frameView, "handleMoveSearchArea")
        val handleResize = findViewByName<TextView>(frameView, "handleResizeSearchArea")
        val btnSave = findViewByName<Button>(frameView, "btnSaveSearchArea")
        val btnReset = findViewByName<Button>(frameView, "btnResetSearchArea")
        val btnCancel = findViewByName<ImageButton>(frameView, "btnCancelSearchArea")

        var areaW = dpToPx(180)
        var areaH = dpToPx(180)
        var areaX = (screenW - areaW) / 2
        var areaY = (screenH - areaH) / 2

        fun updateAreaPositions() {
            areaW = areaW.coerceIn(dpToPx(40), screenW)
            areaH = areaH.coerceIn(dpToPx(40), screenH)
            areaX = areaX.coerceIn(0, screenW - areaW)
            areaY = areaY.coerceIn(0, screenH - areaH)

            viewFrame?.apply {
                layoutParams?.width = areaW
                layoutParams?.height = areaH
                translationX = areaX.toFloat()
                translationY = areaY.toFloat()
                requestLayout()
            }
        }

        val dragListener = object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initX = 0; private var initY = 0
            private var touchX = 0f; private var touchY = 0f
            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initX = areaX; initY = areaY; touchX = event.rawX; touchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        areaX = initX + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()
                        areaY = initY + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()
                        updateAreaPositions()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        }

        handleMove?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)
        viewFrame?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)

        handleResize?.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initW = 0; private var initH = 0
            private var touchX = 0f; private var touchY = 0f
            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initW = areaW; initH = areaH; touchX = event.rawX; touchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        areaW = initW + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()
                        areaH = initH + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()
                        updateAreaPositions()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        btnSave?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            val selectedRect = Rect(areaX, areaY, areaX + areaW, areaY + areaH)
            safeRemoveView(searchAreaOverlayView)
            searchAreaOverlayView = null
            onAreaSelected(selectedRect)
            Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "📐 Зона поиска зафиксирована!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        btnReset?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            safeRemoveView(searchAreaOverlayView)
            searchAreaOverlayView = null
            onAreaSelected(null)
            Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "🌐 Зона поиска сброшена на весь экран", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        btnCancel?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            safeRemoveView(searchAreaOverlayView)
            searchAreaOverlayView = null
        }

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            getOverlayType(), WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        )

        searchAreaOverlayView = frameView
        safeAddView(frameView, params)
        frameView.post { updateAreaPositions() }
    }

    fun showScreenLockOverlay() {
        if (screenLockOverlayView != null) return

        vibrateFeedback(30L)
        val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
        val lockCard = LinearLayout(contextThemeWrapper).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#E60D1117"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(16).toFloat()
                setStroke(dpToPx(2f), Color.parseColor("#F04438"))
            }
            val pad = dpToPx(12)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
            elevation = dpToPx(16).toFloat()
        }

        val tvLockMsg = TextView(this).apply {
            text = "🔒 ЭКРАН ЗАБЛОКИРОВАН\nЗажмите кнопку ниже на 2 сек для разблокировки"
            setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#F04438"))
            textSize = 12f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        }
        lockCard.addView(tvLockMsg)

        val btnUnlock = Button(this).apply {
            text = "🔓 УДЕРЖИВАЙТЕ ДЛЯ РАЗБЛОКИРОВКИ"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 10f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
        }
        lockCard.addView(btnUnlock, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, dpToPx(38)).apply {
            topMargin = dpToPx(8)
        })

        btnUnlock.setOnLongClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(50L)
            safeRemoveView(screenLockOverlayView)
            screenLockOverlayView = null
            Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "🔓 Экран разблокирован!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            true
        }

        val lockParams = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        )

        screenLockOverlayView = lockCard
        safeAddView(screenLockOverlayView, lockParams)
        Toast.makeText(this, "🔒 Защита от случайных нажатий включена!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    private fun spawnEndTargetAtPosition(config: ActionConfig, posX: Float, posY: Float) {
        val endView = LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.floating_target_end, null)
        val tvNumEnd = endView.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumberEnd)
        tvNumEnd.text = "${config.id}E"
        tvNumEnd.visibility = if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE

        val sizePx = dpToPx(36)
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            sizePx, sizePx, getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = (posX - sizePx / 2f).toInt()
            y = (posY - sizePx / 2f).toInt()
        }

        endView.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initialX = 0
            private var initialY = 0
            private var initialTouchX = 0f
            private var initialTouchY = 0f

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (isPlaying) return false

                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initialX = params.x
                        initialY = params.y
                        initialTouchX = event.rawX
                        initialTouchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
                        val endSize = if (endView.width > 0) endView.width else dpToPx(36)
                        params.x = (initialX + (event.rawX - initialTouchX).toInt()).coerceIn(
                            0,
                            displayMetrics.widthPixels - endSize
                        )
                        params.y = (initialY + (event.rawY - initialTouchY).toInt()).coerceIn(
                            0,
                            displayMetrics.heightPixels - endSize
                        )
                        safeUpdateViewLayout(endView, params)
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        v.performClick()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        if (isRecording) {
            endView.visibility = View.INVISIBLE
        }

        config.endView = endView
        safeAddView(endView, params)
    }

    private var tutorialCardView: View? = null
    private var currentTutorialStep = 0
    private var isTutorialActive = false
    private var btnCaptureAnimator: android.animation.ObjectAnimator? = null

    private fun startPulseAnimation(view: View?) {
        if (view == null) return
        if (btnCaptureAnimator?.target === view) return
        stopPulseAnimation(btnCaptureAnimator?.target as? View)

        btnCaptureAnimator = android.animation.ObjectAnimator.ofPropertyValuesHolder(
            view,
            android.animation.PropertyValuesHolder.ofFloat(View.SCALE_X, 1.0f, 1.25f, 1.0f),
            android.animation.PropertyValuesHolder.ofFloat(View.SCALE_Y, 1.0f, 1.25f, 1.0f)
        ).apply {
            duration = 800
            repeatCount = android.animation.ObjectAnimator.INFINITE
            start()
        }
    }

    private fun stopPulseAnimation(view: View?) {
        btnCaptureAnimator?.cancel()
        btnCaptureAnimator = null
        view?.scaleX = 1.0f
        view?.scaleY = 1.0f
    }

    private fun hideTutorial() {
        clearButtonHighlights()
        isTutorialActive = false
        if (tutorialCardView != null) {
            safeRemoveView(tutorialCardView)
            tutorialCardView = null
        }
        val btnCapturePool =
            controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnCapturePool") }
        val btnPlay = controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnPlay") }
        stopPulseAnimation(btnCapturePool)
        stopPulseAnimation(btnPlay)
    }

    private var executionThread: Thread? = null

    private fun startExecutionLoop() {
        if (executionThread != null) return
        acquireWakeLock()
        executionThread = Thread {
            var currentIndex = 0
            while (isPlaying && actionsList.isNotEmpty()) {
                if (currentIndex >= actionsList.size) currentIndex = 0
                val action = actionsList.getOrNull(currentIndex) ?: break
                val repeats = if (action.repeatCount <= 0) Int.MAX_VALUE else action.repeatCount

                for (r in 0 until repeats) {
                    if (!isPlaying) break
                    try {
                        Thread.sleep(action.delay)
                    } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
                        break
                    }
                    if (!isPlaying) break

                    when (action.type) {
                        ActionType.CLICK -> {
                            var clickX = 0f;
                            var clickY = 0f
                            val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
                            uiExecutor.execute {
                                val loc = IntArray(2)
                                action.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
                                val size =
                                    if (action.startView.width > 0) action.startView.width else (36 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
                                clickX =
                                    loc[0] + size / 2f + if (action.randomRadius > 0) kotlin.random.Random.nextInt(
                                        -action.randomRadius,
                                        action.randomRadius + 1
                                    ) else 0
                                clickY =
                                    loc[1] + size / 2f + if (action.randomRadius > 0) kotlin.random.Random.nextInt(
                                        -action.randomRadius,
                                        action.randomRadius + 1
                                    ) else 0
                                latch.countDown()
                            }
                            try {
                                latch.await()
                            } catch (_: Exception) {
                            }
                            showClickVisualizer(clickX, clickY)
                            performClick(clickX, clickY, globalClickDurationMs)
                        }

                        ActionType.LONG_PRESS -> {
                            var clickX = 0f;
                            var clickY = 0f
                            val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
                            uiExecutor.execute {
                                val loc = IntArray(2)
                                action.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
                                val size =
                                    if (action.startView.width > 0) action.startView.width else (36 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
                                clickX = loc[0] + size / 2f; clickY = loc[1] + size / 2f
                                latch.countDown()
                            }
                            try {
                                latch.await()
                            } catch (_: Exception) {
                            }
                            showClickVisualizer(clickX, clickY)
                            performClick(clickX, clickY, action.holdDuration)
                        }

                        ActionType.SWIPE -> {
                            if (action.endView != null) {
                                var startX = 0f;
                                var startY = 0f;
                                var endX = 0f;
                                var endY = 0f
                                val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
                                uiExecutor.execute {
                                    val loc1 = IntArray(2)
                                    action.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc1)
                                    val loc2 = IntArray(2)
                                    action.endView!!.getLocationOnScreen(loc2)
                                    val size =
                                        if (action.startView.width > 0) action.startView.width else (36 * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
                                    startX = loc1[0] + size / 2f; startY = loc1[1] + size / 2f
                                    endX = loc2[0] + size / 2f; endY = loc2[1] + size / 2f
                                    latch.countDown()
                                }
                                try {
                                    latch.await()
                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                }

                                val builder = GestureDescription.Builder()
                                val snapPath = Path().apply {
                                    moveTo(startX, startY)
                                    lineTo(endX, endY)
                                }
                                val snapStroke =
                                    GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(snapPath, 0L, 50L)
                                builder.addStroke(snapStroke)

                                val holdPath = Path().apply {
                                    moveTo(endX, endY)
                                    lineTo(endX, endY)
                                }
                                val holdDuration = action.holdDuration.coerceAtLeast(60L)
                                val holdStroke = GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(
                                    holdPath,
                                    50L,
                                    (holdDuration - 50L).coerceAtLeast(1L)
                                )
                                builder.addStroke(holdStroke)

                                dispatchGesture(builder.build(), null, null)
                            }
                        }

                        ActionType.PATH -> {
                            if (action.pathPoints.isNotEmpty()) {
                                val latch = CountDownLatch(1)
                                uiExecutor.execute { latch.countDown() }
                                try {
                                    latch.await()
                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                }

                                val builder = GestureDescription.Builder()
                                val path = Path()
                                val firstP = action.pathPoints.first()
                                path.moveTo(firstP.x, firstP.y)
                                for (i in 1 until action.pathPoints.size) {
                                    path.lineTo(action.pathPoints[i].x, action.pathPoints[i].y)
                                }
                                val stroke = GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(
                                    path,
                                    0L,
                                    action.holdDuration.coerceAtLeast(100L)
                                )
                                builder.addStroke(stroke)
                                dispatchGesture(builder.build(), null, null)
                            }
                        }

                        ActionType.TRIGGER -> {
                            executeAiTriggerSequence(action)
                        }
                    }
                }
                currentIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % actionsList.size
            }
            isPlaying = false
            uiExecutor.execute {
                stopActionScanningVisual()
                val playBtn = controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnPlay") }
                playBtn?.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_play)
                setTargetsTouchable(true)
            }
        }
        executionThread?.start()
    }

    private fun stopExecutionLoop() {
        isPlaying = false
        releaseWakeLock()
        executionThread?.interrupt()
        executionThread = null
        stopActionScanningVisual()
    }

    fun showControlPanel() {
        if (controlPanelView != null) {
            controlPanelView?.alpha = 1.0f
            controlPanelView?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            setTargetsTouchable(true)
            return
        }

        val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
        controlPanelView =
            LayoutInflater.from(contextThemeWrapper).inflate(R.layout.floating_control_panel, null)

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            x = 100
            y = 300
        }

        val handleDrag = findViewByName<TextView>(controlPanelView!!, "handleDrag")
        val layoutMainRow = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(controlPanelView!!, "layoutMainRow")
        val layoutSubMenu = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(controlPanelView!!, "layoutSubMenu")
        val btnSingleBubble = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnSingleBubble")

        var panelCycleMode = 0

        fun applyPanelCycleState(mode: Int) {
            panelCycleMode = mode % 3
            when (panelCycleMode) {
                0 -> {
                    layoutMainRow?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    layoutSubMenu?.visibility = View.GONE
                    btnSingleBubble?.visibility = View.GONE
                }

                1 -> {
                    layoutMainRow?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    layoutSubMenu?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    btnSingleBubble?.visibility = View.GONE
                }

                2 -> {
                    layoutMainRow?.visibility = View.GONE
                    layoutSubMenu?.visibility = View.GONE
                    btnSingleBubble?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                }
            }

            controlPanelView?.let { panel ->
                val lp = panel.layoutParams as? WindowManager.LayoutParams ?: params
                lp.width = WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
                lp.height = WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
                safeUpdateViewLayout(panel, lp)
            }
        }

        val dragListener = object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initialX = 0
            private var initialY = 0
            private var initialTouchX = 0f
            private var initialTouchY = 0f

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (isPlaying) return false
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initialX = params.x
                        initialY = params.y
                        initialTouchX = event.rawX
                        initialTouchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
                        val maxX = displayMetrics.widthPixels - dpToPx(100)
                        val maxY = displayMetrics.heightPixels - dpToPx(50)

                        params.x =
                            (initialX + (event.rawX - initialTouchX).toInt()).coerceIn(0, maxX)
                        params.y =
                            (initialY + (event.rawY - initialTouchY).toInt()).coerceIn(0, maxY)
                        safeUpdateViewLayout(controlPanelView, params)
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        v.performClick()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        }

        handleDrag?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)

        btnSingleBubble?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            applyPanelCycleState(0)
        }

        btnSingleBubble?.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var startX = 0
            private var startY = 0
            private var touchX = 0f
            private var touchY = 0f
            private var isMoved = false

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (isPlaying) return false
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        startX = params.x
                        startY = params.y
                        touchX = event.rawX
                        touchY = event.rawY
                        isMoved = false
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val dx = abs(event.rawX - touchX)
                        val dy = abs(event.rawY - touchY)
                        if (dx > 8 || dy > 8) {
                            isMoved = true
                            val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
                            params.x = (startX + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()).coerceIn(
                                0,
                                displayMetrics.widthPixels - dpToPx(50)
                            )
                            params.y = (startY + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()).coerceIn(
                                0,
                                displayMetrics.heightPixels - dpToPx(50)
                            )
                            safeUpdateViewLayout(controlPanelView, params)
                        }
                        return true
                    }

                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                        if (!isMoved) {
                            v.performClick()
                        }
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        val btnPlay = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnPlay")
        val btnAdd = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnAdd")
        val subMenuContainer = layoutSubMenu as? LinearLayout
        if (subMenuContainer != null && controlPanelView?.findViewWithTag<View>("btnScreenLockDyn") == null) {
            val btnLock = Button(this).apply {
                tag = "btnScreenLockDyn"
                text = "🔒"
                textSize = 15f
                val size = dpToPx(38)
                layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(size, size).apply {
                    marginEnd = dpToPx(4)
                }
                background = AppCompatResources.getDrawable(this@MyAutoClickService, R.drawable.btn_premium_secondary)
                setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
                setOnClickListener {
                    showScreenLockOverlay()
                }
            }
            subMenuContainer.addView(btnLock, 0)
        }
        if (subMenuContainer != null && controlPanelView?.findViewWithTag<View>("btnJoystickDyn") == null) {
            val btnJoystick = Button(this).apply {
                id = View.generateViewId()
                tag = "btnJoystickDyn"
                text = "🕹"
                textSize = 15f
                val size = dpToPx(38)
                layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(size, size).apply {
                    marginEnd = dpToPx(4)
                }
                background = AppCompatResources.getDrawable(this@MyAutoClickService, R.drawable.btn_premium_secondary)
                setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
                setOnClickListener {
                    vibrateFeedback(30L)
                    showJoystickManipulator()
                }
            }
            subMenuContainer.addView(btnJoystick, 2)
        }

            val btnClearAll = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnClearAll")
        val btnToggleMenu = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnToggleMenu")
        val btnRecord = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnRecord")
        val btnLoadScript = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnLoadScript")
        val btnClose = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnClose")
        val btnHideNumbers = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnHideNumbers")
        val btnGlobalSettings = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnGlobalSettings")
        val btnCapturePool = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnCapturePool")
        val btnHelpTutorial = findViewByName<ImageButton>(controlPanelView!!, "btnHelpTutorial")

        btnHideNumbers?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            isNumbersHidden = !isNumbersHidden
            btnHideNumbers.setImageResource(if (isNumbersHidden) R.drawable.ic_eye_off else R.drawable.ic_eye)
            actionsList.forEach { act ->
                act.startView.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumber)?.visibility =
                    if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE
                act.endView?.findViewById<TextView>(R.id.tvTargetNumberEnd)?.visibility =
                    if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE
            }
        }

        btnGlobalSettings?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            showGlobalSettingsDialog()
        }

        btnGlobalSettings?.setOnLongClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(50L)
            showJoystickManipulator()
            true
        }

        btnCapturePool?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            showCaptureFrame()
        }

        btnHelpTutorial?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            isTutorialActive = true
            currentTutorialStep = 0
            showTutorialCard()
        }

        btnPlay?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            isPlaying = !isPlaying
            setTargetsTouchable(!isPlaying)
            btnPlay.setImageResource(if (isPlaying) R.drawable.ic_pause else R.drawable.ic_play)
            Toast.makeText(this, if (isPlaying) "▶ Запущено" else "⏸ Пауза", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT)
                .show()
            if (isPlaying) {
                startExecutionLoop()
            } else {
                stopExecutionLoop()
            }
        }

        btnAdd?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            showAddActionMenu()
        }

        btnRecord?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            if (isRecording) {
                stopOverlayRecording()
            } else {
                startOverlayRecording()
                Toast.makeText(this, "🔴 Запись включена! Нажимайте по экрану.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT)
                    .show()
            }
        }

        btnLoadScript?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            showScriptsDialog()
        }

        btnClearAll?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            actionsList.forEach {
                safeRemoveView(it.startView)
                it.endView?.let { ev -> safeRemoveView(ev) }
            }
            actionsList.clear()
            Toast.makeText(this, "🗑 Все шаги очищены", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }

        btnToggleMenu?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(25L)
            applyPanelCycleState(panelCycleMode + 1)
        }

        btnClose?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            hideControlPanel()
        }

        safeAddView(controlPanelView, params)
        if (isTutorialActive) showTutorialCard()
    }

    private fun safeUpdateViewLayout(view: View?, params: WindowManager.LayoutParams) {
        if (view == null || view.parent == null) return
        try {
            windowManager.updateViewLayout(view, params)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    private fun safeRemoveView(view: View?) {
        if (view == null || view.parent == null) return
        try {
            logAppEvent(this, "WINDOW", "safeRemoveView: ${view.javaClass.simpleName}")
            windowManager.removeView(view)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    private fun safeAddView(view: View?, params: WindowManager.LayoutParams) {
        if (view == null) return
        try {
            logAppEvent(this, "WINDOW", "safeAddView: ${view.javaClass.simpleName} (${params.width}x${params.height}) at (${params.x}, ${params.y})")
            windowManager.addView(view, params)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAppEvent(this, "ERROR", "safeAddView FAILED for ${view.javaClass.simpleName}: ${e.message}")
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

        private fun dpToPx(dp: Int): Int {
        return (dp * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
    }

    private fun dpToPx(dp: Float): Int {
        return (dp * resources.displayMetrics.density).toInt()
    }

    private fun getOverlayType(): Int {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_ACCESSIBILITY_OVERLAY
        } else {
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.TYPE_PHONE
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
    }

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected()
        instance = this
        windowManager = getSystemService(WINDOW_SERVICE) as WindowManager
        loadAllTemplatesFromDisk()
    }

    override fun onUnbind(intent: Intent?): Boolean {
        instance = null
        return super.onUnbind(intent)
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        stopExecutionLoop()
        hideControlPanel(false)
        instance = null
        bgScannerExecutor.shutdown()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {}

    override fun onInterrupt() {}

    override fun onKeyEvent(event: android.view.KeyEvent?): Boolean {
        if (event?.action == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
            if (event.keyCode == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_VOLUME_DOWN) {
                if (isPlaying) {
                    uiExecutor.execute {
                        vibrateFeedback(40L)
                        stopExecutionLoop()
                        val playBtn = controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnPlay") }
                        playBtn?.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_play)
                        Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "⏹ Экстренная остановка (Громкость -)", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                    return true
                }
            }
        }
        return super.onKeyEvent(event)
    }

    fun performClick(x: Float, y: Float, duration: Long = globalClickDurationMs) {
        performClickWithCallback(x, y, duration, null)
    }

    fun performClickWithCallback(
        x: Float,
        y: Float,
        duration: Long = globalClickDurationMs,
        onComplete: ((Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
    ) {
        val path = Path().apply { moveTo(x, y) }
        val stroke = GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0, duration)
        val gesture = GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(stroke).build()

        var isDone = false
        fun finish(success: Boolean) {
            if (!isDone) {
                isDone = true
                onComplete?.invoke(success)
            }
        }

        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ finish(false) }, duration + 150L)

        val res = dispatchGesture(gesture, object : GestureResultCallback() {
            override fun onCompleted(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    super.onCompleted(gestureDescription)
                finish(true)
            }

            override fun onCancelled(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    super.onCancelled(gestureDescription)
                finish(false)
            }
        }, null)

        if (!res) finish(false)
    }

    fun performSwipe(
        startX: Float,
        startY: Float,
        endX: Float,
        endY: Float,
        duration: Long = globalSwipeDurationMs
    ) {
        performSwipeWithCallback(startX, startY, endX, endY, duration, null)
    }

    fun performSwipeWithCallback(
        startX: Float,
        startY: Float,
        endX: Float,
        endY: Float,
        duration: Long = globalSwipeDurationMs,
        onComplete: ((Boolean) -> Unit)? = null
    ) {
        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(startX, startY)
            lineTo(endX, endY)
        }
        val stroke = GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0, duration)
        val gesture = GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(stroke).build()

        var isDone = false
        fun finish(success: Boolean) {
            if (!isDone) {
                isDone = true
                onComplete?.invoke(success)
            }
        }

        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({ finish(false) }, duration + 150L)

        val res = dispatchGesture(gesture, object : GestureResultCallback() {
            override fun onCompleted(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    super.onCompleted(gestureDescription)
                finish(true)
            }

            override fun onCancelled(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    super.onCancelled(gestureDescription)
                finish(false)
            }
        }, null)

        if (!res) finish(false)
    }

    fun exportScriptWithTemplates(context: Context, scriptName: String) {
        try {
            val scriptsDir = File(context.filesDir, "scripts")
            val scriptFile = File(scriptsDir, "$scriptName.json")
            if (!scriptFile.exists()) return

            val zipFile = File(context.externalCacheDir ?: context.cacheDir, "$scriptName.zip")
            val zos = ZipOutputStream(FileOutputStream(zipFile))

            zos.putNextEntry(ZipEntry("scripts/$scriptName.json"))
            zos.write(scriptFile.readBytes())
            zos.closeEntry()

            val jsonArray = JSONArray(scriptFile.readText())
            val templatePathsToZip = HashSet<String>()

            for (i in 0 until jsonArray.length()) {
                val obj = jsonArray.getJSONObject(i)
                val idx = obj.optInt("selectedTemplateIndex", -1)
                if (idx in globalTemplatesNames.indices) {
                    templatePathsToZip.add(globalTemplatesNames[idx])
                }
            }

            templatePathsToZip.forEach { maskPath ->
                val maskFile = File(maskPath)
                if (maskFile.exists()) {
                    val dateFolder = maskFile.parentFile?.name ?: "default"

                    zos.putNextEntry(ZipEntry("templates/$dateFolder/${maskFile.name}"))
                    zos.write(maskFile.readBytes())
                    zos.closeEntry()

                    val metaFile = getTemplateMetadataFile(maskPath)
                    if (metaFile.exists()) {
                        zos.putNextEntry(ZipEntry("templates/$dateFolder/${metaFile.name}"))
                        zos.write(metaFile.readBytes())
                        zos.closeEntry()
                    }
                }
            }

            zos.close()

            val uri =
                FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", zipFile)
            val shareIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "application/zip"
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }
            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(shareIntent, "Экспортировать").apply {
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            })
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(context, e)
        }
    }

    fun saveScriptByName(name: String) {
        try {
            val jsonArray = JSONArray()

            val headerObj = JSONObject().apply {
                put("isHeaderSpecs", true)
                put("deviceSpecs", getDeviceSpecsJson())
            }
            jsonArray.put(headerObj)

            for (action in actionsList) {
                val obj = JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", action.id)
                    put("type", action.type.name)
                    put("delay", action.delay)
                    put("repeatCount", action.repeatCount)
                    put("holdDuration", action.holdDuration)
                    put("randomRadius", action.randomRadius)
                    put("selectedTemplateIndex", action.selectedTemplateIndex)
                    put("clickAiTarget", action.clickAiTarget)
                    put("aiTimeoutSeconds", action.aiTimeoutSeconds)
                    put("similarityPercent", action.similarityPercent)
                    put("targetScriptToLoad", action.targetScriptToLoad)
                    put("jumpToStepOnMatch", action.jumpToStepOnMatch)
                    put("isFastMode", action.isFastMode)
                    if (action.selectedTemplateIndex in globalTemplatesNames.indices) {
                        put("templatePath", globalTemplatesNames[action.selectedTemplateIndex])
                    } else if (action.templatePath.isNotEmpty()) {
                        put("templatePath", action.templatePath)
                    }

                    if (action.type == ActionType.PATH && action.pathPoints.isNotEmpty()) {
                        val ptsArray = JSONArray()
                        action.pathPoints.forEach { p ->
                            ptsArray.put(JSONObject().apply {
                                put("x", p.x.toDouble())
                                put("y", p.y.toDouble())
                            })
                        }
                        put("pathPoints", ptsArray)
                    }

                    val loc = IntArray(2)
                    action.startView.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
                    put("x", loc[0])
                    put("y", loc[1])
                    if (action.endView != null) {
                        val endLoc = IntArray(2)
                        action.endView!!.getLocationOnScreen(endLoc)
                        put("endX", endLoc[0])
                        put("endY", endLoc[1])
                    }
                }
                jsonArray.put(obj)
            }

            val dir = File(filesDir, "scripts")
            if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()
            val scriptFile = File(dir, "${name}.json")
            FileOutputStream(scriptFile).use { out ->
                out.write(jsonArray.toString().toByteArray())
            }
            Toast.makeText(this, "Сценарий '$name' сохранен!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    fun loadScriptByName(name: String) {
        try {
            val dir = File(filesDir, "scripts")
            val scriptFile = File(dir, "${name}.json")
            if (!scriptFile.exists()) return

            val jsonArray = JSONArray(scriptFile.readText())
            val dm = resources.displayMetrics
            val curW = dm.widthPixels
            val curH = dm.heightPixels
            val curIsLandscape = curW > curH

            var uniformScale = 1.0f
            var offsetX = 0f
            var offsetY = 0f

            var startIndex = 0
            if (jsonArray.length() > 0) {
                val firstObj = jsonArray.getJSONObject(0)
                if (firstObj.optBoolean("isHeaderSpecs", false)) {
                    startIndex = 1
                    val devSpecs = firstObj.optJSONObject("deviceSpecs")
                    var srcW = devSpecs?.optInt("screenWidth", curW) ?: curW
                    var srcH = devSpecs?.optInt("screenHeight", curH) ?: curH
                    val srcIsLandscape = devSpecs?.optBoolean("isLandscape", srcW > srcH) ?: (srcW > srcH)

                    // 🛡 Orientation Guard: авто-своп если ориентации не совпадают
                    if (srcIsLandscape != curIsLandscape) {
                        val temp = srcW
                        srcW = srcH
                        srcH = temp
                    }

                    if (srcW > 0 && srcH > 0) {
                        val sX = curW.toFloat() / srcW.toFloat()
                        val sY = curH.toFloat() / srcH.toFloat()
                        uniformScale = kotlin.math.min(sX, sY)

                        val scaledW = srcW * uniformScale
                        val scaledH = srcH * uniformScale
                        offsetX = (curW - scaledW) / 2f
                        offsetY = (curH - scaledH) / 2f
                    }
                }
            }

            uiExecutor.execute {
                actionsList.forEach { act ->
                    safeRemoveView(act.startView)
                    act.endView?.let { ev -> safeRemoveView(ev) }
                }
                actionsList.clear()

                for (i in startIndex until jsonArray.length()) {
                    val obj = jsonArray.getJSONObject(i)
                    val typeStr = obj.optString("type", "CLICK")
                    val type = try {
                        ActionType.valueOf(typeStr)
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        ActionType.CLICK
                    }

                    val origX = obj.optInt("x", 500)
                    val origY = obj.optInt("y", 500)
                    val x = (origX * uniformScale + offsetX).coerceIn(0f, curW.toFloat())
                    val y = (origY * uniformScale + offsetY).coerceIn(0f, curH.toFloat())
                    val delay = obj.optLong("delay", 1000L)

                    addNewActionAtPosition(x, y, delay, type, -1)

                    val newAction = actionsList.last()
                    newAction.holdDuration = obj.optLong("holdDuration", 1000L)
                    newAction.repeatCount = obj.optInt("repeatCount", 1)
                    newAction.randomRadius = obj.optInt("randomRadius", 0)
                    newAction.selectedTemplateIndex = obj.optInt("selectedTemplateIndex", -1)
                    newAction.clickAiTarget = obj.optBoolean("clickAiTarget", true)
                    newAction.aiTimeoutSeconds = obj.optInt("aiTimeoutSeconds", 2)
                    newAction.similarityPercent = obj.optInt("similarityPercent", 80)
                    newAction.targetScriptToLoad = obj.optString("targetScriptToLoad", "")
                    newAction.jumpToStepOnMatch = obj.optInt("jumpToStepOnMatch", -1)
                    newAction.isFastMode = obj.optBoolean("isFastMode", true)
                    newAction.templatePath = obj.optString("templatePath", "")
                    if (newAction.templatePath.isNotEmpty()) {
                        val foundIdx =
                            globalTemplatesNames.indexOfFirst { it.endsWith(File(newAction.templatePath).name) }
                        if (foundIdx != -1) {
                            newAction.selectedTemplateIndex = foundIdx
                        }
                    }

                    if (obj.has("pathPoints")) {
                        val ptsArray = obj.getJSONArray("pathPoints")
                        for (j in 0 until ptsArray.length()) {
                            val pObj = ptsArray.getJSONObject(j)
                            val origPx = pObj.getDouble("x").toFloat()
                            val origPy = pObj.getDouble("y").toFloat()
                            val px = (origPx * uniformScale + offsetX).coerceIn(0f, curW.toFloat())
                            val py = (origPy * uniformScale + offsetY).coerceIn(0f, curH.toFloat())
                            newAction.pathPoints.add(android.graphics.PointF(px, py))
                        }
                    }

                    if (obj.has("endX") && obj.has("endY")) {
                        val origEndX = obj.getInt("endX")
                        val origEndY = obj.getInt("endY")
                        val endX = (origEndX * uniformScale + offsetX).coerceIn(0f, curW.toFloat())
                        val endY = (origEndY * uniformScale + offsetY).coerceIn(0f, curH.toFloat())
                        spawnEndTargetAtPosition(newAction, endX, endY)
                    }
                }
                Toast.makeText(
                    this,
                    "Сценарий '$name' успешно адаптирован под ваш экран!",
                    Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
                ).show()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    fun loadAllTemplatesFromDisk() {
        try {
            purgeOldTrashTemplates()
            globalTemplates.forEach {
                try {
                    it.recycle()
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                }
            }
            globalTemplates.clear()
            globalTemplatesNames.clear()
            val baseDir = File(filesDir, "templates")
            if (baseDir.exists()) {
                val allMasks = mutableListOf<File>()

                baseDir.walkTopDown().forEach { file ->
                    if (file.isFile && file.name.startsWith("mask_") && file.name.endsWith(".png")) {
                        allMasks.add(file)
                    }
                }
                allMasks.sortBy { it.name }

                allMasks.forEach { file ->
                    BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath)?.let { bmp ->
                        globalTemplates.add(bmp)
                        globalTemplatesNames.add(file.absolutePath)
                    }
                }
            }
            logAppEvent(
                this,
                "TEMPLATES",
                "Успешно загружено ИИ-шаблонов из памяти: ${globalTemplates.size}"
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    fun hideControlPanel(openMainApp: Boolean = true) {
        isPlaying = false
        stopOverlayRecording()
        actionsList.forEach { action ->
            safeRemoveView(action.startView)
            action.endView?.let { safeRemoveView(it) }
        }
        actionsList.clear()

        if (joystickOverlayView != null) {
            safeRemoveView(joystickOverlayView)
            joystickOverlayView = null
        }

        controlPanelView?.let { safeRemoveView(it); controlPanelView = null }

        if (openMainApp) {
            try {
                val intent = Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)
                }
                startActivity(intent)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                logError(this, e)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun purgeOldTrashTemplates() {
        try {
            val trashDir = File(filesDir, "trash_templates")
            if (trashDir.exists()) {
                val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                val sevenDaysMs = 7L * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000L
                trashDir.walkTopDown().forEach { file ->
                    if (file.isFile && (now - file.lastModified() > sevenDaysMs)) {
                        file.delete()
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    fun moveTemplateToTrash(index: Int) {
        if (index !in globalTemplatesNames.indices) return
        try {
            val maskPath = globalTemplatesNames[index]
            val maskFile = File(maskPath)
            if (maskFile.exists()) {
                val dateFolder = maskFile.parentFile?.name ?: "default"
                val targetTrashDir =
                    File(File(filesDir, "trash_templates"), dateFolder).apply { mkdirs() }
                maskFile.renameTo(File(targetTrashDir, maskFile.name))
            }
            globalTemplates.removeAt(index)
            globalTemplatesNames.removeAt(index)
            Toast.makeText(this, "🗑 Шаблон перемещен в корзину", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    fun startOverlayRecording() {
        isRecording = true
        isInjectingGesture = false
        lastRecordedTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

        if (recordOverlayView == null) {
            recordOverlayView = View(this).apply {
                setBackgroundColor(0x01000000)
            }
        }

        val overlayParams = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        )

        var downX = 0f
        var downY = 0f
        var downTime = 0L

        recordOverlayView?.setOnTouchListener { v, event ->
            if (!isRecording || isInjectingGesture) return@setOnTouchListener false

            val x = event.rawX
            val y = event.rawY

            when (event.action) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    downX = x
                    downY = y
                    downTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                    return@setOnTouchListener true
                }

                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    return@setOnTouchListener true
                }

                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                    v.performClick()
                    val upTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
                    val dist = hypot((x - downX).toDouble(), (y - downY).toDouble())
                    val delay = (downTime - lastRecordedTime).coerceIn(80L, 5000L)
                    val gestureHoldDuration = (upTime - downTime).coerceIn(80L, 3000L)
                    lastRecordedTime = downTime

                    isInjectingGesture = true
                    safeRemoveView(recordOverlayView)

                    Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                        if (dist > 60) {
                            addNewActionAtPosition(downX, downY, delay, ActionType.SWIPE, -1)
                            val currentConfig = actionsList.last()
                            currentConfig.holdDuration = gestureHoldDuration
                            spawnEndTargetAtPosition(currentConfig, x, y)

                            performSwipeWithCallback(
                                downX,
                                downY,
                                x,
                                y,
                                duration = gestureHoldDuration
                            ) { success ->
                                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                                    safeAddView(recordOverlayView, overlayParams)
                                    isInjectingGesture = false
                                }, 30L)
                            }
                        } else {
                            addNewActionAtPosition(downX, downY, delay, ActionType.CLICK, -1)

                            performClickWithCallback(downX, downY, duration = 30L) { success ->
                                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                                    safeAddView(recordOverlayView, overlayParams)
                                    isInjectingGesture = false
