                                }, 30L)
                            }
                        }
                    }, 25L)

                    return@setOnTouchListener true
                }
            }
            false
        }

        controlPanelView?.visibility = View.GONE
        safeAddView(recordOverlayView, overlayParams)
        showRecordBar()
    }

    fun stopOverlayRecording() {
        isRecording = false
        isInjectingGesture = false

        if (recordOverlayView != null) {
            safeRemoveView(recordOverlayView)
            recordOverlayView = null
        }

        if (recordBarView != null) {
            safeRemoveView(recordBarView)
            recordBarView = null
        }

        actionsList.forEach { act ->
            act.startView.visibility = if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE
            act.endView?.visibility = if (isNumbersHidden) View.INVISIBLE else View.VISIBLE
        }

        controlPanelView?.alpha = 1.0f
        controlPanelView?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
    }

    private fun showAddActionMenu() {
        val dialogView = LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.dialog_add_action, null)
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply { gravity = Gravity.CENTER }

        val btnClick = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnAddClick")
        val btnSwipe = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnAddSwipe")
        val btnAi = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnAddTrigger")
        val btnCancel = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnCancelAdd")

        val spawnOffset = (actionsList.size % 8) * dpToPx(24).toFloat()
        val spawnX = 350f + spawnOffset
        val spawnY = 350f + spawnOffset

        btnClick?.setOnClickListener {
            addNewActionAtPosition(spawnX, spawnY, 1000L, ActionType.CLICK, -1)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
        }
        btnSwipe?.setOnClickListener {
            addNewActionAtPosition(spawnX, spawnY, 1000L, ActionType.SWIPE, -1)
            spawnEndTargetAtPosition(actionsList.last(), spawnX + 100f, spawnY + 100f)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
        }
        btnAi?.setOnClickListener {
            addNewActionAtPosition(spawnX, spawnY, 1000L, ActionType.TRIGGER, 0)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
        }
        btnCancel?.setOnClickListener { safeRemoveView(dialogView) }
        safeAddView(dialogView, params)
    }

    private fun showSaveRecordingDialog() {
        val dialogView = LayoutInflater.from(this).inflate(R.layout.dialog_save_recording, null)
        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply { gravity = Gravity.CENTER }

        val etName = findViewByName<EditText>(dialogView, "etRecordScriptName")
        val btnSave = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnSaveRecordScript")
        val btnSkip = findViewByName<Button>(dialogView, "btnSkipRecordScript")

        btnSave?.setOnClickListener {
            val name = etName?.text?.toString()?.trim() ?: ""
            val finalName = if (name.isEmpty()) "Запись_${System.currentTimeMillis()}" else name
            saveScriptByName(finalName)
            safeRemoveView(dialogView)
        }
        btnSkip?.setOnClickListener { safeRemoveView(dialogView) }
        safeAddView(dialogView, params)
    }

    private fun showRecordBar() {
        if (recordBarView != null) return

        val btnStop = Button(this).apply {
            text = "⏹ СТОП ЗАПИСИ"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 13f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(getColor(R.color.red_close))
            setPadding(dpToPx(16), dpToPx(8), dpToPx(16), dpToPx(8))
        }

        val barParams = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
            y = dpToPx(40)
        }

        btnStop.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(30L)
            stopOverlayRecording()
            Toast.makeText(
                this,
                "Запись остановлена. Записано шагов: ${actionsList.size}",
                Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
            ).show()
            if (actionsList.isNotEmpty()) {
                showSaveRecordingDialog()
            }
        }

        recordBarView = btnStop
        safeAddView(recordBarView, barParams)
    }

    fun removeHighlightOverlay() {
        highlightHandler.removeCallbacks(hideHighlightRunnable)
        if (highlightOverlayView != null) {
            safeRemoveView(highlightOverlayView)
            highlightOverlayView = null
        }
    }

    private fun executeAiTriggerSequence(action: ActionConfig) {
        val searchIndices = if (action.multiTemplateIndices.isNotEmpty()) {
            action.multiTemplateIndices
        } else if (action.selectedTemplateIndex in globalTemplates.indices) {
            arrayListOf(action.selectedTemplateIndex)
        } else {
            ArrayList(globalTemplates.indices.toList())
        }

        if (searchIndices.isEmpty()) return

        val timeoutMs = if (action.aiTimeoutSeconds > 0) action.aiTimeoutSeconds * 1000L else Long.MAX_VALUE
        val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val loopIntervalMs = action.checkInterval.coerceIn(50L, 5000L)
        val requiredScore = (action.similarityPercent.coerceIn(50, 100)) / 100f

        startActionScanningVisual(action)

        while (isPlaying && (System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime < timeoutMs)) {
            val screenshotLatch = CountDownLatch(1)
            var bestTournamentWinner: Pair<MatchCandidate, Int>? = null

            setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(false)

            takeScreenshot(
                Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY,
                bgScannerExecutor,
                object : TakeScreenshotCallback {
                    override fun onSuccess(screenshotResult: ScreenshotResult) {
                        setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                        val hwBuffer = screenshotResult.hardwareBuffer
                        try {
                            val hwBitmap = Bitmap.wrapHardwareBuffer(hwBuffer, screenshotResult.colorSpace)
                            val softwareBitmap = hwBitmap?.copy(Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888, false)
                            if (softwareBitmap != null) {
                                val tournamentPool = mutableListOf<Pair<MatchCandidate, Int>>()

                                // ⚡ Турнирный мультипоиск: сначала проверяет сохраненную позицию, затем быстрый отсев
                                for (idx in searchIndices) {
                                    if (!isPlaying) break
                                    if (idx !in globalTemplates.indices) continue

                                    val template = globalTemplates[idx]
                                    val templatePath = globalTemplatesNames[idx]
                                    val meta = loadTemplateMetadata(templatePath)

                                    val matches = findTemplateCandidatesCoarseFine(
                                        softwareBitmap,
                                        template,
                                        meta,
                                        templatePath,
                                        minSimilarityPercent = action.similarityPercent
                                    )

                                    for (m in matches) {
                                        if (m.score >= requiredScore) {
                                            tournamentPool.add(Pair(m, idx))
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                if (tournamentPool.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    tournamentPool.sortByDescending { it.first.score }
                                    bestTournamentWinner = tournamentPool.first()
                                }

                                softwareBitmap.recycle()
                            }
                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                            logError(this@MyAutoClickService, e)
                        } finally {
                            try { hwBuffer.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                            screenshotLatch.countDown()
                        }
                    }

                    override fun onFailure(errorCode: Int) {
                        setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                        screenshotLatch.countDown()
                    }
                })

            try {
                screenshotLatch.await(3000, java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
            if (!isPlaying) break

            bestTournamentWinner?.let { (winnerCandidate, winnerTemplateIdx) ->
                val jitterX = if (action.randomRadius > 0) kotlin.random.Random.nextInt(-action.randomRadius, action.randomRadius + 1) else 0
                val jitterY = if (action.randomRadius > 0) kotlin.random.Random.nextInt(-action.randomRadius, action.randomRadius + 1) else 0
                val clickX = winnerCandidate.point.x.toFloat() + jitterX
                val clickY = winnerCandidate.point.y.toFloat() + jitterY

                showClickVisualizer(clickX, clickY)
                showHighlightBox(winnerCandidate.rect)

                if (action.clickAiTarget) {
                    performClick(clickX, clickY, globalClickDurationMs)
                }

                if (action.multiTemplateIndices.isEmpty() && action.isFastMode) {
                    return@executeAiTriggerSequence
                }
            }

            try {
                Thread.sleep(loopIntervalMs)
            } catch (_: InterruptedException) {
                break
            }
        }

        stopActionScanningVisual()
    }

    private var highlightBoxView: View? = null

        private fun showHighlightBox(rect: Rect) {
        uiExecutor.execute {
            if (highlightBoxView != null) {
                safeRemoveView(highlightBoxView)
                highlightBoxView = null
            }

            highlightBoxView = View(this).apply {
                background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                    setStroke(dpToPx(2.5f), Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
                    setColor(Color.parseColor("#2500F5D4"))
                    cornerRadius = dpToPx(6).toFloat()
                }
                scaleX = 1.15f
                scaleY = 1.15f
                alpha = 0f
            }

            val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
                rect.width(),
                rect.height(),
                getOverlayType(),
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
                PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
            ).apply {
                gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
                x = rect.left
                y = rect.top
            }

            safeAddView(highlightBoxView, params)

            highlightBoxView?.animate()
                ?.scaleX(1.0f)
                ?.scaleY(1.0f)
                ?.alpha(1.0f)
                ?.setDuration(180)
                ?.setInterpolator(android.view.animation.DecelerateInterpolator())
                ?.withEndAction {
                    Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                        highlightBoxView?.animate()?.alpha(0f)?.setDuration(250)?.withEndAction {
                            safeRemoveView(highlightBoxView)
                            highlightBoxView = null
                        }?.start()
                    }, 1400L)
                }
                ?.start()
        }
    }

    private fun getDeviceSpecsJson(): JSONObject {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics
        val isLandscape = dm.widthPixels > dm.heightPixels
        return JSONObject().apply {
            put("screenWidth", dm.widthPixels)
            put("screenHeight", dm.heightPixels)
            put("densityDpi", dm.densityDpi)
            put("density", dm.density.toDouble())
            put("isLandscape", isLandscape)
        }
    }

    fun saveTemplateMetadata(
        maskPath: String,
        similarityPct: Int,
        isPinned: Boolean = true,
        localArea: Boolean = false,
        customArea: Boolean = false,
        sX: Int = 0,
        sY: Int = 0,
        sW: Int = 0,
        sH: Int = 0,
        mX: Int = 0,
        mY: Int = 0,
        mW: Int = 0,
        mH: Int = 0
    ) {
        try {
            if (maskPath.isEmpty()) return
            val metaFile = getTemplateMetadataFile(maskPath)
            val json = if (metaFile.exists()) JSONObject(metaFile.readText()) else JSONObject()

            json.put("similarityPercent", similarityPct)
            json.put("isPinned", isPinned)
            json.put("sourceDevice", getDeviceSpecsJson())
            json.put("searchInCapturedArea", localArea)
            json.put("customSearchArea", customArea)
            json.put("searchAreaX", sX)
            json.put("searchAreaY", sY)
            json.put("searchAreaW", sW)
            json.put("searchAreaH", sH)
            if (mW > 0) {
                json.put("maskX", mX)
                json.put("maskY", mY)
                json.put("maskW", mW)
                json.put("maskH", mH)
            }
            FileOutputStream(metaFile).use { out -> out.write(json.toString().toByteArray()) }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        }
    }

    private fun findCandidatesForCreation(
        screenBitmap: Bitmap,
        template: Bitmap,
        cropX: Int,
        cropY: Int,
        cropW: Int,
        cropH: Int
    ): MutableList<MatchCandidate> {
        val candidates = mutableListOf<MatchCandidate>()

        val safeX = cropX.coerceIn(0, (screenBitmap.width - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val safeY = cropY.coerceIn(0, (screenBitmap.height - 1).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val safeW = cropW.coerceIn(1, screenBitmap.width - safeX)
        val safeH = cropH.coerceIn(1, screenBitmap.height - safeY)

        val originRect = Rect(safeX, safeY, safeX + safeW, safeY + safeH)
        val originPoint = Point(safeX + safeW / 2, safeY + safeH / 2)
        val originCandidate = MatchCandidate(originPoint, originRect, 1.0f)
        candidates.add(originCandidate)

        val candidatesFromSearch = findTemplateCandidatesCoarseFine(screenBitmap, template, null)
        for (cand in candidatesFromSearch) {
            if (!Rect.intersects(cand.rect, originRect) && candidates.size < 6) {
                candidates.add(cand)
            }
        }

        return candidates
    }

    private fun highlightButton(button: View?) {
        clearButtonHighlights()
        if (button == null) return

        highlightedButtonAnim = android.animation.ObjectAnimator.ofPropertyValuesHolder(
            button,
            android.animation.PropertyValuesHolder.ofFloat(View.SCALE_X, 1.0f, 1.35f, 1.0f),
            android.animation.PropertyValuesHolder.ofFloat(View.SCALE_Y, 1.0f, 1.35f, 1.0f)
        ).apply {
            duration = 500
            repeatCount = android.animation.ObjectAnimator.INFINITE
            start()
        }
    }

    private fun clearButtonHighlights() {
        highlightedButtonAnim?.cancel()
        highlightedButtonAnim = null
        val buttons = listOf(
            "btnPlay",
            "btnAdd",
            "btnCapturePool",
            "btnHelpTutorial",
            "btnToggleMenu",
            "btnClearAll",
            "btnRecord",
            "btnGlobalSettings",
            "btnLoadScript",
            "btnHideNumbers",
            "btnClose"
        )
        controlPanelView?.let { panel ->
            buttons.forEach { idName ->
                findViewByName<View>(panel, idName)?.apply {
                    scaleX = 1.0f
                    scaleY = 1.0f
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun positionTutorialCardSide() {
        val tutorial = tutorialCardView ?: return
        val panel = controlPanelView ?: return

        // 🛡 Принудительно запрашиваем реальные размеры панели с учетом раскрытия 2-й строки меню
        panel.measure(
            View.MeasureSpec.makeMeasureSpec(0, View.MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED),
            View.MeasureSpec.makeMeasureSpec(0, View.MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED)
        )

        val panelLoc = IntArray(2)
        panel.getLocationOnScreen(panelLoc)
        val panelX = panelLoc[0]
        val panelY = panelLoc[1]
        val panelW = panel.width.coerceAtLeast(panel.measuredWidth).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(180))
        val panelH = panel.height.coerceAtLeast(panel.measuredHeight).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(50))

        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
        val screenW = displayMetrics.widthPixels
        val screenH = displayMetrics.heightPixels

        val cardW = dpToPx(240)
        val cardH = dpToPx(160)

        val spaceRight = screenW - (panelX + panelW)
        val spaceLeft = panelX
        val spaceAbove = panelY - dpToPx(40)

        var cardX: Int
        var cardY: Int

        // 🎯 Умное позиционирование: Показываем сбоку или СВЕРХУ панели, чтобы никогда не закрывать 2-ю строку кнопок
        if (spaceRight >= cardW + dpToPx(12)) {
            cardX = panelX + panelW + dpToPx(12)
            cardY = panelY.coerceIn(dpToPx(40), (screenH - cardH - dpToPx(40)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(40)))
        } else if (spaceLeft >= cardW + dpToPx(12)) {
            cardX = panelX - cardW - dpToPx(12)
            cardY = panelY.coerceIn(dpToPx(40), (screenH - cardH - dpToPx(40)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(40)))
        } else if (spaceAbove >= cardH + dpToPx(12)) {
            cardX = panelX.coerceIn(dpToPx(8), (screenW - cardW - dpToPx(8)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(8)))
            cardY = panelY - cardH - dpToPx(12)
        } else {
            cardX = panelX.coerceIn(dpToPx(8), (screenW - cardW - dpToPx(8)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(8)))
            cardY = (panelY + panelH + dpToPx(12)).coerceAtMost(screenH - cardH - dpToPx(20))
        }

        val lp = tutorial.layoutParams as? WindowManager.LayoutParams ?: return
        lp.gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
        lp.x = cardX
        lp.y = cardY
        safeUpdateViewLayout(tutorial, lp)
    }

    private fun updateTutorialContent() {
        if (tutorialCardView == null) return

        val layoutSubMenu =
            controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<LinearLayout>(it, "layoutSubMenu") }
        if (currentTutorialStep >= 5) {
            layoutSubMenu?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
        }

        val targetButton: View? = when (currentTutorialStep) {
            0 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnPlay") }
            1 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnAdd") }
            2 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnCapturePool") }
            3 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnHelpTutorial") }
            4 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnToggleMenu") }
            5 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnClearAll") }
            6 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnRecord") }
            7 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnGlobalSettings") }
            8 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnLoadScript") }
            9 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnHideNumbers") }
            10 -> controlPanelView?.let { findViewByName<ImageButton>(it, "btnClose") }
            else -> null
        }

        highlightButton(targetButton)
        startPulseAnimation(targetButton)

        positionTutorialCardSide()

        val tvTitle = findViewByName<TextView>(tutorialCardView!!, "tvTutTitle")
        val tvDesc = findViewByName<TextView>(tutorialCardView!!, "tvTutDesc")
        val btnNext = findViewByName<Button>(tutorialCardView!!, "btnTutNext")

        when (currentTutorialStep) {
            0 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "1/11: Запуск [▶]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Запускает и останавливает выполнение всех созданных шагов."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            1 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "2/11: Добавить [+]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Добавляет новый обычный клик, свайп или ИИ-триггер."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            2 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "3/11: ИИ-Сканер [📸]"
                tvDesc?.text =
                    "Открывает прицел для вырезания картинки с экрана и создания ИИ-маски."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            3 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "4/11: Справка [❓]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Повторный вызов этого интерактивного обучения по кнопкам."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            4 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "5/11: Меню [☰]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Разворачивает и сворачивает дополнительную панель инструментов."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            5 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "6/11: Очистить [🗑]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Удаляет абсолютно все мишени и шаги с экрана."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            6 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "7/11: Запись [🔴]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Включает живую запись ваших кликов и свайпов прямо по экрану!"
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            7 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "8/11: Глобальные настройки [⚙️]"
                tvDesc?.text =
                    "Настройки кликов/свайпов. 💡 ЗАЖМИТЕ шестерёнку на 1 сек, чтобы открыть АКТИВНЫЙ ДЖОЙСТИК!"
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            8 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "9/11: Скрипты [📁]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Сохранение текущей схемы шагов в файл и загрузка сохраненных."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            9 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "10/11: Глаз [👁]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Скрывает или показывает бейджи с номерами поверх шагов."
                btnNext?.text = "Далее ►"
            }

            10 -> {
                tvTitle?.text = "11/11: Закрыть [❌]"
                tvDesc?.text = "Выход из панели кликера и возвращение в главное меню."
                btnNext?.text = "Завершить ✔"
            }
        }
    }

    private fun showTutorialCard() {
        if (!isTutorialActive) return
        if (tutorialCardView != null) {
            tutorialCardView?.visibility = View.VISIBLE
            updateTutorialContent()
            return
        }

        val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
        tutorialCardView =
            LayoutInflater.from(contextThemeWrapper).inflate(R.layout.floating_tutorial_card, null)

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        )

        val btnPrev = findViewByName<Button>(tutorialCardView!!, "btnTutPrev")
        val btnNext = findViewByName<Button>(tutorialCardView!!, "btnTutNext")
        val btnSkip = findViewByName<Button>(tutorialCardView!!, "btnTutSkip")

        btnPrev?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            if (currentTutorialStep > 0) {
                currentTutorialStep--
                updateTutorialContent()
            }
        }

        btnNext?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            if (currentTutorialStep < 10) {
                currentTutorialStep++
                updateTutorialContent()
            } else {
                hideTutorial()
            }
        }

        btnSkip?.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            hideTutorial()
        }

        safeAddView(tutorialCardView, params)
        updateTutorialContent()
    }

    private fun showCaptureFrame() {
        try {
            if (captureFrameView != null) {
                safeRemoveView(captureFrameView)
                captureFrameView = null
            }

            hideControlPanelTemporarily(30000L)
            setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(false)

            val contextThemeWrapper = ContextThemeWrapper(this, R.style.Theme_AutoTap)
            captureFrameView = LayoutInflater.from(contextThemeWrapper).inflate(R.layout.floating_capture_frame, null)

            val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                getOverlayType(),
                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
                PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
            ).apply {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                    layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES
                }
            }

            val layoutTopBar = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(captureFrameView!!, "layoutTopBar")
            val layoutBottomBar = findViewByName<LinearLayout>(captureFrameView!!, "layoutBottomBar")
            val captureSquare = findViewByName<View>(captureFrameView!!, "captureSquare")
            val handleMove = findViewByName<TextView>(captureFrameView!!, "handleMoveFrame")
            val handleResize = findViewByName<TextView>(captureFrameView!!, "handleResize")
            val btnCancel = findViewByName<ImageButton>(captureFrameView!!, "btnCancelCapture")
            val btnCapture = findViewByName<ImageButton>(captureFrameView!!, "btnDoCapture")
            val btnToggleShape = findViewByName<Button>(captureFrameView!!, "btnToggleCaptureShape")

            val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
            val screenW = displayMetrics.widthPixels
            val screenH = displayMetrics.heightPixels

            var frameW = dpToPx(36)
            var frameH = dpToPx(36)
            var frameX = (screenW - frameW) / 2
            var frameY = (screenH - frameH) / 2
            var isCircleShape = true

            fun updateFramePositions() {
                frameW = frameW.coerceIn(dpToPx(14), screenW)
                frameH = frameH.coerceIn(dpToPx(14), screenH)
                frameX = frameX.coerceIn(0, screenW - frameW)
                frameY = frameY.coerceIn(-dpToPx(10), screenH - frameH)

                captureSquare?.apply {
                    layoutParams?.width = frameW
                    layoutParams?.height = frameH
                    translationX = frameX.toFloat()
                    translationY = frameY.toFloat()
                    requestLayout()
                }

                val topBarW = layoutTopBar?.width?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: dpToPx(150)
                val topBarH = layoutTopBar?.height?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: dpToPx(40)
                val botBarW = layoutBottomBar?.width?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: dpToPx(80)
                val botBarH = layoutBottomBar?.height?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: dpToPx(36)

                val safeTop = dpToPx(30)
                var topY = frameY - topBarH - dpToPx(6)
                var botY = frameY + frameH + dpToPx(6)

                if (topY < safeTop) {
                    topY = frameY + frameH + dpToPx(6)
                    botY = topY + topBarH + dpToPx(6)
                }

                if (botY + botBarH > screenH - dpToPx(20)) {
                    if (topY > frameY) {
                        topY = frameY + dpToPx(6)
                        botY = frameY + frameH - botBarH - dpToPx(6)
                    } else {
                        botY = frameY - botBarH - dpToPx(6)
                    }
                }

                val topBarX = (frameX + (frameW - topBarW) / 2).coerceIn(dpToPx(8), screenW - topBarW - dpToPx(8))
                val botBarX = (frameX + (frameW - botBarW) / 2).coerceIn(dpToPx(8), screenW - botBarW - dpToPx(8))

                layoutTopBar?.apply { translationX = topBarX.toFloat(); translationY = topY.toFloat() }
                layoutBottomBar?.apply { translationX = botBarX.toFloat(); translationY = botY.toFloat() }
            }

            btnToggleShape?.setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                isCircleShape = !isCircleShape
                btnToggleShape.text = if (isCircleShape) "🔘" else "🔲"
                captureSquare?.setBackgroundResource(if (isCircleShape) R.drawable.border_capture else R.drawable.border_capture_square)
            }

            if (captureSquare != null) {
                android.animation.ObjectAnimator.ofFloat(captureSquare, "alpha", 1f, 0.4f, 1f).apply {
                    duration = 1000; repeatCount = android.animation.ObjectAnimator.INFINITE; start()
                }
            }

            val dragListener = object : View.OnTouchListener {
                private var initFrameX = 0; private var initFrameY = 0
                private var touchX = 0f; private var touchY = 0f
                private var isMoved = false

                override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                    when (event.action) {
                        MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                            initFrameX = frameX; initFrameY = frameY
                            touchX = event.rawX; touchY = event.rawY
                            isMoved = false
                            return true
                        }
                        MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                            if (abs(event.rawX - touchX) > 8 || abs(event.rawY - touchY) > 8) {
                                isMoved = true
                                frameX = initFrameX + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()
                                frameY = initFrameY + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()
                                updateFramePositions()
                            }
                            return true
                        }
                        MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
                            if (!isMoved) {
                                val containerLoc = IntArray(2)
                                captureFrameView?.getLocationOnScreen(containerLoc)
                                val localX = event.rawX - containerLoc[0]
                                val localY = event.rawY - containerLoc[1]

                                frameX = (localX - frameW / 2f).toInt()
                                frameY = (localY - frameH / 2f).toInt()
                                updateFramePositions()
                                vibrateFeedback(25L)
                            }
                            return true
                        }
                    }
                    return false
                }
            }

            handleMove?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)
            captureSquare?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)
            captureFrameView?.setOnTouchListener(dragListener)

            handleResize?.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
                private var initW = 0; private var initH = 0
                private var touchX = 0f; private var touchY = 0f

                override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                    when (event.action) {
                        MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                            initW = frameW; initH = frameH
                            touchX = event.rawX; touchY = event.rawY
                            return true
                        }
                        MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                            frameW = initW + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()
                            frameH = initH + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()
                            updateFramePositions()
                            return true
                        }
                    }
                    return false
                }
            })

            btnCancel?.setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(25L)
                safeRemoveView(captureFrameView)
                captureFrameView = null
                setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                restoreControlPanelImmediately()
            }

            btnCapture?.setOnClickListener {
                try {
                    vibrateFeedback(50L)
                    val cropX = frameX.coerceAtLeast(0)
                    val cropY = frameY.coerceAtLeast(0)
                    val cropW = frameW
                    val cropH = frameH

                    safeRemoveView(captureFrameView)
                    captureFrameView = null

                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                        var isCallbackReceived = false
                        val screenshotTimeoutRunnable = Runnable {
                            if (!isCallbackReceived) {
                                setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                                restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                                Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "⚠️ Таймаут ответа ОС!", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                            }
                        }
                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(screenshotTimeoutRunnable, 3000L)

                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                            try {
                                takeScreenshot(Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY, bgScannerExecutor, object : TakeScreenshotCallback {
                                    override fun onSuccess(screenshotResult: ScreenshotResult) {
                                        isCallbackReceived = true
                                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).removeCallbacks(screenshotTimeoutRunnable)
                                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true) }

                                        val hwBuffer = screenshotResult.hardwareBuffer
                                        try {
                                            val hwBitmap = Bitmap.wrapHardwareBuffer(hwBuffer, screenshotResult.colorSpace)
                                            val softwareBitmap = hwBitmap?.copy(Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888, false)

                                            if (softwareBitmap != null) {
                                                val safeW = cropW.coerceIn(16, softwareBitmap.width)
                                                val safeH = cropH.coerceIn(16, softwareBitmap.height)
                                                val safeX = cropX.coerceIn(0, (softwareBitmap.width - safeW).coerceAtLeast(0))
                                                val safeY = cropY.coerceIn(0, (softwareBitmap.height - safeH).coerceAtLeast(0))

                                                val cropped = Bitmap.createBitmap(softwareBitmap, safeX, safeY, safeW, safeH)
                                                val smartMask = generateSmartMask(cropped, isCircleShape, 20)
                                                val candRefined = refineAndTrimSmartMask(smartMask, safeX, safeY)
                                                val candMaskBitmap = candRefined.trimmedBitmap

                                                val ts = System.currentTimeMillis()
                                                val targetDir = File(filesDir, "templates/default").apply { mkdirs() }
                                                val tPath = File(targetDir, "mask_$ts.png").absolutePath
                                                val fPath = File(targetDir, "full_$ts.png").absolutePath

                                                saveBitmapToPng(candMaskBitmap, File(tPath))
                                                saveBitmapToPng(softwareBitmap, File(fPath))

                                                val metaFile = getTemplateMetadataFile(tPath)
                                                val meta = JSONObject().apply {
                                                    put("similarityPercent", 85)
                                                    put("bgTolerance", 20)
                                                    put("originX", candRefined.tightX)
                                                    put("originY", candRefined.tightY)
                                                    put("originW", candRefined.tightW)
                                                    put("originH", candRefined.tightH)
                                                    put("isCircleShape", isCircleShape)
                                                    put("isContourMode", true)
                                                    put("isAnimMode", false)
                                                    put("isSubpixelMode", false)
                                                }

                                                val smartData = analyzeTemplate(candMaskBitmap)
                                                for (key in smartData.keys()) {
                                                    meta.put(key, smartData.get(key))
                                                }
                                                FileOutputStream(metaFile).use { out -> out.write(meta.toString().toByteArray()) }

                                                val initialCandidates = findCandidatesForCreation(
                                                    softwareBitmap,
                                                    candMaskBitmap,
                                                    safeX,
                                                    safeY,
                                                    safeW,
                                                    safeH
                                                )

                                                uiExecutor.execute {
                                                    try {
                                                        loadAllTemplatesFromDisk()
                                                        showCandidatesSelectionOverlay(
                                                            softwareBitmap,
                                                            cropped, // Передаем оригинальный кадр для корректного замера bgTolerance
                                                            initialCandidates,
                                                            isCircleShape,
                                                            null,
                                                            tPath
                                                        )
                                                        candMaskBitmap.recycle() // Своевременно освобождаем временный растр
                                                    } catch (uiEx: Exception) {
                                                        logError(this@MyAutoClickService, uiEx)
                                                        restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                                                    }
                                                }

                                                cropped.recycle()
                                            } else {
                                                uiExecutor.execute { restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                                            }
                                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                                            logError(this@MyAutoClickService, e)
                                            uiExecutor.execute { restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                                        } finally {
                                            try { hwBuffer.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                                        }
                                    }

                                    override fun onFailure(errorCode: Int) {
                                        isCallbackReceived = true
                                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).removeCallbacks(screenshotTimeoutRunnable)
                                        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true); restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                                    }
                                })
                            } catch (sendEx: Exception) {
                                isCallbackReceived = true
                                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).removeCallbacks(screenshotTimeoutRunnable)
                                logError(this@MyAutoClickService, sendEx)
                                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true); restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                            }
                        }, 300L)
                    }
                } catch (btnEx: Exception) {
                    logError(this@MyAutoClickService, btnEx)
                    restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                }
            }

            safeAddView(captureFrameView, params)
            captureFrameView?.post { updateFramePositions() }
        } catch (frameEx: Exception) {
            logError(this@MyAutoClickService, frameEx)
            restoreControlPanelImmediately()
        }
    }

    private fun analyzeTemplate(template: Bitmap): JSONObject {
        val w = template.width
        val h = template.height
        val area = w * h
        val aspectRatio = w.toFloat() / h.toFloat()

        val type = when {
            h <= 10 && w >= 60 -> TemplateType.THIN_HORIZONTAL
            w <= 10 && h >= 60 -> TemplateType.THIN_VERTICAL
            aspectRatio >= 3.0f -> TemplateType.WIDE
            area <= 400 -> TemplateType.MICRO
            area <= 3600 -> TemplateType.SMALL
            area <= 10000 -> TemplateType.MEDIUM
            area <= 40000 -> TemplateType.LARGE
            else -> TemplateType.HUGE
        }

        val sobelWeight =
            if (type == TemplateType.SMALL || type == TemplateType.MICRO || type == TemplateType.THIN_HORIZONTAL || type == TemplateType.THIN_VERTICAL) 0.70 else 0.35
        val step = if (type == TemplateType.LARGE || type == TemplateType.HUGE) 8 else 6

        return JSONObject().apply {
            put("templateType", type.name)
            put("width", w)
            put("height", h)
            put("aspectRatio", aspectRatio)
            put("searchStrategy", JSONObject().apply {
                put("scanStepX", step)
                put("scanStepY", step)
                put("fineStepX", 1)
                put("fineStepY", 1)
            })
            put("matching", JSONObject().apply {
                put("mode", "HYBRID")
                put("similarity", 0.80)
                put("sobelWeight", sobelWeight)
                put("pixelWeight", 1.0 - sobelWeight)
            })
        }
    }

                private fun findTemplateCandidatesCoarseFine(
        screenBitmap: Bitmap,
        template: Bitmap,
        meta: JSONObject?,
        templatePath: String = "",
        minSimilarityPercent: Int = 80,
        isCancelled: () -> Boolean = { false }
    ): List<MatchCandidate> {
        val dm = resources.displayMetrics
        val curW = dm.widthPixels
        val curH = dm.heightPixels
        val curIsLandscape = curW > curH

        // 1. Извлечение параметров устройства и Orientation Guard
        val sourceDevice = meta?.optJSONObject("sourceDevice")
        var srcW = sourceDevice?.optInt("screenWidth", curW) ?: curW
        var srcH = sourceDevice?.optInt("screenHeight", curH) ?: curH
        val srcIsLandscape = sourceDevice?.optBoolean("isLandscape", srcW > srcH) ?: (srcW > srcH)

        if (srcIsLandscape != curIsLandscape) {
            val temp = srcW
            srcW = srcH
            srcH = temp
        }

        // 2. Изотропное масштабирование без искажения геометрии
        val sX = curW.toFloat() / srcW.toFloat()
        val sY = curH.toFloat() / srcH.toFloat()
        val uniformScale = kotlin.math.min(sX, sY)

        val scaledTemplate = if (abs(uniformScale - 1.0f) > 0.04f) {
            val newTw = (template.width * uniformScale).toInt().coerceAtLeast(6)
            val newTh = (template.height * uniformScale).toInt().coerceAtLeast(6)
            Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(template, newTw, newTh, true)
        } else template

        val sw = screenBitmap.width
        val sh = screenBitmap.height
        val tw = scaledTemplate.width
        val th = scaledTemplate.height

        if (tw > sw || th > sh) {
            if (scaledTemplate != template) scaledTemplate.recycle()
            return emptyList()
        }

        // 3. Анализ структуры шаблона (Геометрия, Тонкие линии, Текст)
        val area = tw * th
        val isThinHorizontal = th <= 12 && tw >= 30
        val isThinVertical = tw <= 12 && th >= 30
        val isMicro = area <= 500
        val isSmall = area in 501..3600
        val isLarge = area > 25000

        val isContourMode = meta?.optBoolean("isContourMode", true) ?: true
        val isAnimMode = meta?.optBoolean("isAnimMode", false) ?: false
        val isSubpixelMode = (meta?.optBoolean("isSubpixelMode", false) ?: false) || isThinHorizontal || isThinVertical || isMicro

        // Адаптивный допуск по цвету
        val colorTol = if (isAnimMode) 52 else if (isMicro || isThinHorizontal || isThinVertical) 44 else 36
        val effectivePct = if (minSimilarityPercent > 0) minSimilarityPercent else (meta?.optInt("similarityPercent", 80) ?: 80)
        val userConfiguredScore = (effectivePct.coerceIn(40, 100)) / 100f

        // Буфер пикселей экрана
        val requiredSize = sw * sh
        val currentPool = reusablePixelsPool
        val sPixels = if (currentPool != null && currentPool.size >= requiredSize) {
            currentPool
        } else {
            IntArray(requiredSize).also { reusablePixelsPool = it }
        }
        screenBitmap.getPixels(sPixels, 0, sw, 0, 0, sw, sh)

        // Извлечение пикселей шаблона и расчет контурных граней
        val tPixels = IntArray(tw * th)
        scaledTemplate.getPixels(tPixels, 0, tw, 0, 0, tw, th)

        val maxPixels = tw * th
        val insideDx = IntArray(maxPixels)
        val insideDy = IntArray(maxPixels)
        val insideR = IntArray(maxPixels)
        val insideG = IntArray(maxPixels)
        val insideB = IntArray(maxPixels)
        val insideEdge = BooleanArray(maxPixels)
        var insideCount = 0
        var templateEdgeCount = 0
        var sumX = 0L; var sumY = 0L
        var tInsideLumSum = 0L

        for (ty in 0 until th) {
            for (tx in 0 until tw) {
                val idx = ty * tw + tx
                val c = tPixels[idx]
                val alpha = Color.alpha(c)

                if (alpha > 30) {
                    val r = Color.red(c); val g = Color.green(c); val b = Color.blue(c)
                    insideDx[insideCount] = tx
                    insideDy[insideCount] = ty
                    insideR[insideCount] = r
                    insideG[insideCount] = g
                    insideB[insideCount] = b

                    val lum = (r * 299 + g * 587 + b * 114) / 1000
                    tInsideLumSum += lum
                    sumX += tx; sumY += ty

                    // Определение контурных граней
                    if (tx < tw - 1 && ty < th - 1) {
                        val rightC = tPixels[idx + 1]
                        val downC = tPixels[idx + tw]
                        val lumR = (Color.red(rightC) * 299 + Color.green(rightC) * 587 + Color.blue(rightC) * 114) / 1000
                        val lumD = (Color.red(downC) * 299 + Color.green(downC) * 587 + Color.blue(downC) * 114) / 1000
                        if (abs(lum - lumR) + abs(lum - lumD) > 22) {
                            insideEdge[insideCount] = true
                            templateEdgeCount++
                        }
                    }
                    insideCount++
                }
            }
        }

        if (insideCount < 4) {
            if (scaledTemplate != template) scaledTemplate.recycle()
            return emptyList()
        }

        val centroidOffsetX = (sumX / insideCount).toInt()
        val centroidOffsetY = (sumY / insideCount).toInt()
        val avgTemplateInsideLum = (tInsideLumSum / insideCount).toInt()

        val candidates = mutableListOf<MatchCandidate>()

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // ⚡ УРОВЕНЬ 0: Мгновенный приоритетный поиск в зоне захвата (< 2 мс)
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        val targetZone = if (templatePath.isNotEmpty() && lastMatchedPositions.containsKey(templatePath)) {
            val p = lastMatchedPositions[templatePath]!!
            Rect((p.x - 70).coerceAtLeast(0), (p.y - 70).coerceAtLeast(0), (p.x + 70).coerceAtMost(sw), (p.y + 70).coerceAtMost(sh))
        } else if (meta != null && meta.has("originX")) {
            val ox = (meta.getInt("originX") * uniformScale).toInt()
            val oy = (meta.getInt("originY") * uniformScale).toInt()
            val ow = (meta.optInt("originW", tw) * uniformScale).toInt()
            val oh = (meta.optInt("originH", th) * uniformScale).toInt()
            Rect((ox - 70).coerceAtLeast(0), (oy - 70).coerceAtLeast(0), (ox + ow + 70).coerceAtMost(sw), (oy + oh + 70).coerceAtMost(sh))
        } else null

        if (targetZone != null && !isCancelled()) {
            val fyStart = Math.max(0, targetZone.top)
            val fyEnd = Math.min(sh - th, targetZone.bottom)
            val fxStart = Math.max(0, targetZone.left)
            val fxEnd = Math.min(sw - tw, targetZone.right)

            var bestOriginScore = 0f
            var bestOriginX = fxStart
            var bestOriginY = fyStart

            val originStep = if (isSubpixelMode) 1 else 2

            for (fy in fyStart..fyEnd step originStep) {
                for (fx in fxStart..fxEnd step originStep) {
                    var fMatches = 0
                    var edgeMatches = 0

                    for (k in 0 until insideCount) {
                        val sIdx = (fy + insideDy[k]) * sw + (fx + insideDx[k])
                        val sColor = sPixels[sIdx]
                        val dr = abs(Color.red(sColor) - insideR[k])
                        val dg = abs(Color.green(sColor) - insideG[k])
                        val db = abs(Color.blue(sColor) - insideB[k])

                        if (dr + dg + db <= (colorTol * 2.2)) {
                            fMatches++
                            if (insideEdge[k]) edgeMatches++
                        }
                    }

                    val colorScore = fMatches.toFloat() / insideCount.toFloat()
                    val edgeScore = if (templateEdgeCount > 0) edgeMatches.toFloat() / templateEdgeCount.toFloat() else colorScore
                    val hybridScore = if (isContourMode && templateEdgeCount > 6) (colorScore * 0.65f + edgeScore * 0.35f) else colorScore

                    if (hybridScore > bestOriginScore) {
                        bestOriginScore = hybridScore
                        bestOriginX = fx
                        bestOriginY = fy
                    }
                }
            }

            if (bestOriginScore >= (userConfiguredScore * 0.92f)) {
                val rect = Rect(bestOriginX, bestOriginY, bestOriginX + tw, bestOriginY + th)
                val targetClickPoint = Point(bestOriginX + centroidOffsetX, bestOriginY + centroidOffsetY)
                if (templatePath.isNotEmpty()) lastMatchedPositions[templatePath] = targetClickPoint
                candidates.add(MatchCandidate(targetClickPoint, rect, bestOriginScore))
                if (scaledTemplate != template) scaledTemplate.recycle()
                return candidates
            }
        }

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // ⚡ УРОВЕНЬ 1: Выбор опорных якорных точек (Anchor Points)
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        val sampleSize = if (isMicro || isThinHorizontal || isThinVertical) min(12, insideCount) else min(24, insideCount)
        val sampleStep = (insideCount / sampleSize).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val sampleDx = IntArray(sampleSize)
        val sampleDy = IntArray(sampleSize)
        val sampleR = IntArray(sampleSize)
        val sampleG = IntArray(sampleSize)
        val sampleB = IntArray(sampleSize)
        var realSampleSize = 0

        var sp = 0
        while (sp < insideCount && realSampleSize < sampleSize) {
            sampleDx[realSampleSize] = insideDx[sp]
            sampleDy[realSampleSize] = insideDy[sp]
            sampleR[realSampleSize] = insideR[sp]
            sampleG[realSampleSize] = insideG[sp]
            sampleB[realSampleSize] = insideB[sp]
            realSampleSize++
            sp += sampleStep
        }

        val scanStep = when {
            isSubpixelMode -> 1
            isMicro -> 2
            isSmall -> 3
            isLarge -> 5
            else -> 4
        }

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // ⚡ УРОВЕНЬ 2: Быстрый отсев + УРОВЕНЬ 3: Яркостная нормализация
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        for (y in 0..(sh - th) step scanStep) {
            if (isCancelled()) break

            for (x in 0..(sw - tw) step scanStep) {
                var fastMatches = 0
                for (s in 0 until realSampleSize) {
                    val sIdx = (y + sampleDy[s]) * sw + (x + sampleDx[s])
                    val sColor = sPixels[sIdx]
                    val dr = abs(Color.red(sColor) - sampleR[s])
                    val dg = abs(Color.green(sColor) - sampleG[s])
                    val db = abs(Color.blue(sColor) - sampleB[s])

                    if (dr + dg + db <= (colorTol * 2.5)) {
                        fastMatches++
                    }
                }

                if (fastMatches < (realSampleSize * 0.50f)) continue

                var candLumSum = 0L
                for (s in 0 until realSampleSize) {
                    val sIdx = (y + sampleDy[s]) * sw + (x + sampleDx[s])
                    val sColor = sPixels[sIdx]
                    candLumSum += (Color.red(sColor) * 299 + Color.green(sColor) * 587 + Color.blue(sColor) * 114) / 1000
                }
                val avgCandLum = (candLumSum / realSampleSize).toInt()
                val lumOffset = if (isAnimMode) 0 else ((avgCandLum - avgTemplateInsideLum) / 2).coerceIn(-25, 25)

                // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                // ⚡ УРОВЕНЬ 4: Тонкое выравнивание (Subpixel Fine Refinement ±1..2 px)
                // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                val fineRadius = if (scanStep > 2) 2 else 1
                val fyStart = Math.max(0, y - fineRadius)
                val fyEnd = Math.min(sh - th, y + fineRadius)
                val fxStart = Math.max(0, x - fineRadius)
                val fxEnd = Math.min(sw - tw, x + fineRadius)

                var bestLocalScore = 0f
                var bestLocalX = x
                var bestLocalY = y

                for (fy in fyStart..fyEnd step 1) {
                    for (fx in fxStart..fxEnd step 1) {
                        var fullMatches = 0
                        var edgeMatches = 0

                        for (k in 0 until insideCount) {
                            val sIdx = (fy + insideDy[k]) * sw + (fx + insideDx[k])
                            val sColor = sPixels[sIdx]

                            val rExp = (insideR[k] + lumOffset).coerceIn(0, 255)
                            val gExp = (insideG[k] + lumOffset).coerceIn(0, 255)
                            val bExp = (insideB[k] + lumOffset).coerceIn(0, 255)

                            val dr = abs(Color.red(sColor) - rExp)
                            val dg = abs(Color.green(sColor) - gExp)
                            val db = abs(Color.blue(sColor) - bExp)

                            if (dr + dg + db <= (colorTol * 2.2)) {
                                fullMatches++
                                if (insideEdge[k]) edgeMatches++
                            }
                        }

                        val colorScore = fullMatches.toFloat() / insideCount.toFloat()
                        val edgeScore = if (templateEdgeCount > 0) edgeMatches.toFloat() / templateEdgeCount.toFloat() else colorScore
                        val totalScore = if (isContourMode && templateEdgeCount > 6) {
                            (colorScore * 0.65f + edgeScore * 0.35f)
                        } else {
                            colorScore
                        }

                        if (totalScore > bestLocalScore) {
                            bestLocalScore = totalScore
                            bestLocalX = fx
                            bestLocalY = fy
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (bestLocalScore >= userConfiguredScore) {
                    val rect = Rect(bestLocalX, bestLocalY, bestLocalX + tw, bestLocalY + th)
                    val targetClickPoint = Point(bestLocalX + centroidOffsetX, bestLocalY + centroidOffsetY)
                    if (templatePath.isNotEmpty()) lastMatchedPositions[templatePath] = targetClickPoint
                    candidates.add(MatchCandidate(targetClickPoint, rect, bestLocalScore))
                }
            }
        }

        if (scaledTemplate != template) scaledTemplate.recycle()
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) return emptyList()

        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // ⚡ УРОВЕНЬ 5: Non-Maximum Suppression (Устранение дубликатов)
        // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        candidates.sortByDescending { it.score }
        val nmsList = mutableListOf<MatchCandidate>()
        for (cand in candidates) {
            if (nmsList.none { Rect.intersects(it.rect, cand.rect) }) {
                nmsList.add(cand)
            }
        }
        return nmsList.take(6)
    }

    private fun saveBitmapToPng(bitmap: Bitmap, file: File) {
        var out: FileOutputStream? = null
        try {
            out = FileOutputStream(file)
            bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, out)
            out.flush()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logError(this, e)
        } finally {
            try {
                out?.close()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
        }
    }

    fun generateConfidenceHeatmap(
        screenBitmap: Bitmap,
        template: Bitmap,
        meta: JSONObject?
    ): Bitmap {
        val downscale = 8
        val sw = (screenBitmap.width / downscale).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val sh = (screenBitmap.height / downscale).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val tw = (template.width / downscale).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val th = (template.height / downscale).coerceAtLeast(1)

        logAppEvent(this, "HEATMAP", "Расчёт тепловой карты сходимости (${sw}x${sh}, шаблон ${tw}x${th})")

        val heatmapBmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(sw, sh, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val smallScreen = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(screenBitmap, sw, sh, false)
        val smallTemplate = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(template, tw, th, false)

        val sPixels = IntArray(sw * sh)
        val tPixels = IntArray(tw * th)
        smallScreen.getPixels(sPixels, 0, sw, 0, 0, sw, sh)
        smallTemplate.getPixels(tPixels, 0, tw, 0, 0, tw, th)

        val heatPixels = IntArray(sw * sh)

        for (y in 0 until (sh - th) step 2) {
            for (x in 0 until (sw - tw) step 2) {
                var matches = 0
                var total = 0
                for (ty in 0 until th step 2) {
                    for (tx in 0 until tw step 2) {
                        val tColor = tPixels[ty * tw + tx]
                        if (Color.alpha(tColor) > 35) {
                            val sColor = sPixels[(y + ty) * sw + (x + tx)]
                            val dr = abs(Color.red(sColor) - Color.red(tColor))
                            val dg = abs(Color.green(sColor) - Color.green(tColor))
                            val db = abs(Color.blue(sColor) - Color.blue(tColor))
                            if (dr + dg + db < 70) matches++
                            total++
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (total > 0) {
                    val score = matches.toFloat() / total.toFloat()
                    if (score > 0.45f) {
                        val color = when {
                            score >= 0.85f -> Color.argb(170, 0, 245, 212)   // Неоновый бирюзовый (Пик)
                            score >= 0.70f -> Color.argb(130, 255, 183, 3)   // Золотой (Близко)
                            else -> Color.argb(80, 88, 166, 255)             // Синий (Слабый фон)
                        }
                        heatPixels[y * sw + x] = color
                        if (x + 1 < sw) heatPixels[y * sw + x + 1] = color
                        if (y + 1 < sh) heatPixels[(y + 1) * sw + x] = color
                        if (x + 1 < sw && y + 1 < sh) heatPixels[(y + 1) * sw + x + 1] = color
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        heatmapBmp.setPixels(heatPixels, 0, sw, 0, 0, sw, sh)
        smallScreen.recycle()
        smallTemplate.recycle()
        val scaledHeatmap = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(heatmapBmp, screenBitmap.width, screenBitmap.height, true)
        heatmapBmp.recycle() // Своевременное освобождение промежуточного растра
        return scaledHeatmap
    }

        private fun refineAndTrimSmartMask(
        smartMask: Bitmap,
        origX: Int,
        origY: Int
    ): RefinedMaskResult {
        val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] Вход. smartMask: ${smartMask.width}x${smartMask.height}")
        val w = smartMask.width
        val h = smartMask.height
        val pixels = IntArray(w * h)
        try {
            smartMask.getPixels(pixels, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] Исключение при getPixels: ${e.message}")
            return RefinedMaskResult(smartMask, origX, origY, w, h)
        }

        var minX = w;
        var minY = h
        var maxX = -1;
        var maxY = -1

        for (y in 0 until h) {
            for (x in 0 until w) {
                if (Color.alpha(pixels[y * w + x]) > 30) {
                    if (x < minX) minX = x
                    if (x > maxX) maxX = x
                    if (y < minY) minY = y
                    if (y > maxY) maxY = y
                }
            }
        }

        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] Расчет контуров. minX: $minX, minY: $minY, maxX: $maxX, maxY: $maxY")

        if (maxX < minX || maxY < minY) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ: Границы непрозрачных пикселей не обнаружены (маска пустая).")
            return RefinedMaskResult(smartMask, origX, origY, w, h)
        }

        minX = minX.coerceAtLeast(0)
        minY = minY.coerceAtLeast(0)
        maxX = maxX.coerceAtMost(w - 1)
        maxY = maxY.coerceAtMost(h - 1)

        val tightW = maxX - minX + 1
        val tightH = maxY - minY + 1
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] Вычисленные размеры обрезки: ${tightW}x${tightH} на оффсете ($minX, $minY)")

        if (tightW <= 4 || tightH <= 4) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] ПРЕДУПРЕЖДЕНИЕ: Слишком малый размер плотного контура (${tightW}x${tightH}). Возврат без обрезки.")
            return RefinedMaskResult(smartMask, origX, origY, w, h)
        }

        val tightBitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(smartMask, minX, minY, tightW, tightH)
        if (tightBitmap != smartMask) {
            smartMask.recycle()
        }
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[refineAndTrimSmartMask] Успешно завершено за ${System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime}ms. tightW: $tightW, tightH: $tightH")
        return RefinedMaskResult(tightBitmap, origX + minX, origY + minY, tightW, tightH)
    }

    private fun generateSmartMask(src: Bitmap, isCircle: Boolean, bgTolerance: Int = 45): Bitmap {
        val startTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Вход. src: ${src.width}x${src.height}, isCircle: $isCircle, bgTolerance: $bgTolerance, isRecycled: ${src.isRecycled}")
        if (src.isRecycled) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] ОШИБКА: Исходный Bitmap утилизирован!")
            return Bitmap.createBitmap(16, 16, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        }
        val w = src.width
        val h = src.height
        val out = Bitmap.createBitmap(w, h, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val pixels = IntArray(w * h)
        try {
            src.getPixels(pixels, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Исключение при getPixels: ${e.message}")
            return out
        }

        val cx = w / 2f
        val cy = h / 2f
        val radius = min(w.toFloat(), h.toFloat()) / 2f

        val step = (Math.max(w, h) / 12).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val bR = IntArray(64); val bG = IntArray(64); val bB = IntArray(64)
        var bCount = 0

        fun addBorderColor(c: Int) {
            if (bCount < 64) {
                bR[bCount] = (c shr 16) and 0xFF
                bG[bCount] = (c shr 8) and 0xFF
                bB[bCount] = c and 0xFF
                bCount++
            }
        }

        for (x in 0 until w step step) {
            addBorderColor(pixels[x])
            addBorderColor(pixels[(h - 1) * w + x])
        }
        for (y in 0 until h step step) {
            addBorderColor(pixels[y * w])
            addBorderColor(pixels[y * w + (w - 1)])
        }

        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Сэмплировано фоновых пикселей (bCount): $bCount")

        var validPixelsCount = 0
        for (y in 0 until h) {
            for (x in 0 until w) {
                val idx = y * w + x
                val pxColor = pixels[idx]
                var alpha = (pxColor ushr 24)

                if (isCircle && hypot((x - cx).toDouble(), (y - cy).toDouble()) > radius) {
                    alpha = 0
                }

                if (alpha > 0) {
                    val pr = (pxColor shr 16) and 0xFF
                    val pg = (pxColor shr 8) and 0xFF
                    val pb = pxColor and 0xFF

                    var isBgPixel = false
                    for (i in 0 until bCount) {
                        if (abs(pr - bR[i]) + abs(pg - bG[i]) + abs(pb - bB[i]) < bgTolerance) {
                            isBgPixel = true
                            break
                        }
                    }

                    if (isBgPixel) alpha = 0
                }

                if (alpha > 0) validPixelsCount++
                pixels[idx] = if (alpha == 0) Color.TRANSPARENT else (alpha shl 24) or (pxColor and 0x00FFFFFF)
            }
        }

        val minRequiredPixels = (w * h) * 0.02
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Обработано. validPixelsCount: $validPixelsCount, миним. лимит (2%): $minRequiredPixels")
        
        if (validPixelsCount < minRequiredPixels) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] validPixelsCount ($validPixelsCount) < minRequiredPixels ($minRequiredPixels). Срабатывает FALLBACK (восстановление оригинальных цветов с обрезкой круга).")
            try {
                src.getPixels(pixels, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Исключение в fallback getPixels: ${e.message}")
            }
            for (y in 0 until h) {
                for (x in 0 until w) {
                    val idx = y * w + x
                    if (isCircle && hypot((x - cx).toDouble(), (y - cy).toDouble()) > radius) {
                        pixels[idx] = Color.TRANSPARENT
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        out.setPixels(pixels, 0, w, 0, 0, w, h)
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[generateSmartMask] Успешно завершено за ${System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime}ms")
        return out
    }

                    private fun showCandidatesSelectionOverlay(
        screenshot: Bitmap,
        rawTemplateBitmap: Bitmap,
        initialCandidates: List<MatchCandidate>,
        isCircleInitial: Boolean,
        existingConfig: ActionConfig?,
        existingTemplatePath: String?
    ) {
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[showCandidatesSelectionOverlay] Вход. screenshot: ${screenshot.width}x${screenshot.height}, rawTemplateBitmap: ${rawTemplateBitmap.width}x${rawTemplateBitmap.height}, кандидатов: ${initialCandidates.size}")

        var currentCandidates = initialCandidates.toMutableList()
        var currentIsCircle = isCircleInitial
        var currentSimilarity = existingConfig?.similarityPercent ?: 85
        var currentBgTolerance = 20 // Устанавливаем безопасный щадящий порог по умолчанию

        var currentIsContourMode = existingConfig?.isContourMode ?: true
        var currentIsAnimMode = existingConfig?.isAnimMode ?: false
        var currentIsSubpixelMode = (existingConfig?.isSubpixelMode ?: false)
        var isHeatmapVisible = false
        var heatmapBitmap: Bitmap? = null

        // 🎯 Сохраняем ссылку на сгенерированную маску для предотвращения утечек ОЗУ
        var currentTrimmedMask: Bitmap? = null

        val updateTrimmedMask = { newMask: Bitmap ->
            currentTrimmedMask?.let {
                if (it != rawTemplateBitmap) {
                    it.recycle()
                }
            }
            currentTrimmedMask = newMask
        }

        val recomputeHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
        var pendingRecomputeRunnable: Runnable? = null

        val displayMetrics = resources.displayMetrics
        val screenW = displayMetrics.widthPixels
        val screenH = displayMetrics.heightPixels

        val overlayView = FrameLayout(this).apply {
            setBackgroundColor(Color.parseColor("#35000000"))
            clipChildren = false
            clipToPadding = false
        }

        // 1. Слой тепловой карты
        val ivHeatmapOverlay = ImageView(this).apply {
            scaleType = ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_XY
            visibility = View.GONE
        }
        overlayView.addView(ivHeatmapOverlay, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

        // 2. Слой кандидатов (Маяки и маски)
        val candidatesContainer = FrameLayout(this).apply {
            clipChildren = false
            clipToPadding = false
        }
        overlayView.addView(candidatesContainer, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

        val titleCard = TextView(this).apply {
            text = if (existingConfig == null) "🎯 Нажмите на подходящее совпадение" else "⚙️ Подтвердите калибровку шага"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 12f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            val pxPad = dpToPx(8)
            setPadding(pxPad * 2, pxPad, pxPad * 2, pxPad)
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#E60D1117"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(8).toFloat()
                setStroke(dpToPx(1.5f), Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
            }
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        }
        overlayView.addView(titleCard, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
            topMargin = dpToPx(28)
        })

        val cardWidthPx = dpToPx(285)
        var isMenuCollapsed = false

        val settingsCard = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#FA0D1117"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(14).toFloat()
                setStroke(dpToPx(1.5f), Color.parseColor("#58A6FF"))
            }
            val cardPad = dpToPx(8)
            setPadding(cardPad, cardPad, cardPad, cardPad)
            elevation = dpToPx(16).toFloat()
        }

        val topHeaderRow = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#21262D"))
                cornerRadius = dpToPx(8).toFloat()
            }
            val p = dpToPx(4)
            setPadding(p * 2, p, p * 2, p)
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(34)).apply {
                bottomMargin = dpToPx(4)
            }
        }

        val handleDrag = TextView(this).apply {
            text = "✥ ⚙️ Параметры маски"
            setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#58A6FF"))
            textSize = 11f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
        }

        val btnCollapse = Button(this).apply {
            text = "▼ Свернуть"

            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))

            text = "🎯 Порог совпадения: $currentSimilarity%"

            textSize = 10f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)

        settingsBody.addView(tvSimLabel)

            max = 50
            progress = (currentSimilarity - 50).coerceIn(0, 50)
            setOnSeekBarChangeListener(object : android.widget.SeekBar.OnSeekBarChangeListener {
                override fun onProgressChanged(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?, progress: Int, fromUser: Boolean) {

                }
                override fun onStartTrackingTouch(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?) {}
                override fun onStopTrackingTouch(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?) {}
