            text = "🎨 Чувствительность фона: $currentBgTolerance"
            setTextColor(Color.parseColor("#FFB703"))
            textSize = 10f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
        }
        settingsBody.addView(tvBgLabel)

        val sbBgTolerance = android.widget.SeekBar(this).apply {
            max = 90
            progress = (currentBgTolerance - 5).coerceIn(0, 90)
            setOnSeekBarChangeListener(object : android.widget.SeekBar.OnSeekBarChangeListener {
                override fun onProgressChanged(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?, progress: Int, fromUser: Boolean) {
                    currentBgTolerance = 5 + progress
                    tvBgLabel.text = "🎨 Чувствительность фона: $currentBgTolerance"
                    if (fromUser) triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef()
                }
                override fun onStartTrackingTouch(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?) {}
                override fun onStopTrackingTouch(seekBar: android.widget.SeekBar?) {}
            })
        }
        settingsBody.addView(sbBgTolerance)

        val modeTogglesRow = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT).apply {
                topMargin = dpToPx(3); bottomMargin = dpToPx(3)
            }
        }

        fun getToggleBgColor(active: Boolean): ColorStateList {
            return ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor(if (active) "#1F6FEB" else "#161B22"))
        }

        val btnContourToggle = Button(this).apply {
            text = if (currentIsContourMode) "⚡Контур ✔" else "⚡Контур ✕"
            textSize = 8.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsContourMode)
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dpToPx(2), 0, dpToPx(2), 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                currentIsContourMode = !currentIsContourMode
                text = if (currentIsContourMode) "⚡Контур ✔" else "⚡Контур ✕"
                backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsContourMode)
                triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef()
            }
        }
        modeTogglesRow.addView(btnContourToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dpToPx(30), 1f).apply { marginEnd = dpToPx(2) })

        val btnAnimToggle = Button(this).apply {
            text = if (currentIsAnimMode) "🔥Аним ✔" else "🔥Аним ✕"
            textSize = 8.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsAnimMode)
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dpToPx(2), 0, dpToPx(2), 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                currentIsAnimMode = !currentIsAnimMode
                text = if (currentIsAnimMode) "🔥Аним ✔" else "🔥Аним ✕"
                backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsAnimMode)
                triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef()
            }
        }
        modeTogglesRow.addView(btnAnimToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dpToPx(30), 1f).apply { marginEnd = dpToPx(2) })

        val btnSubpixelToggle = Button(this).apply {
            text = if (currentIsSubpixelMode) "🎯Субпикс ✔" else "🎯Субпикс ✕"
            textSize = 8.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsSubpixelMode)
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dpToPx(2), 0, dpToPx(2), 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                currentIsSubpixelMode = !currentIsSubpixelMode
                text = if (currentIsSubpixelMode) "🎯Субпикс ✔" else "🎯Субпикс ✕"
                backgroundTintList = getToggleBgColor(currentIsSubpixelMode)
                triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef()
            }
        }
        modeTogglesRow.addView(btnSubpixelToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dpToPx(30), 1.1f))
        settingsBody.addView(modeTogglesRow)

        val btnHeatmapToggle = Button(this).apply {
            text = "🔥 Тепловая карта: ВЫКЛ"
            textSize = 9f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dpToPx(4), 0, dpToPx(4), 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(25L)
                isHeatmapVisible = !isHeatmapVisible
                text = if (isHeatmapVisible) "🔥 Тепловая карта: ВКЛ" else "🔥 Тепловая карта: ВЫКЛ"
                backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor(if (isHeatmapVisible) "#1F6FEB" else "#161B22"))
                if (isHeatmapVisible) {
                    if (heatmapBitmap == null && !screenshot.isRecycled && !rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled) {
                        bgScannerExecutor.execute {
                            val hm = generateConfidenceHeatmap(screenshot, rawTemplateBitmap, null)
                            uiExecutor.execute {
                                heatmapBitmap = hm
                                ivHeatmapOverlay.setImageBitmap(hm)
                                ivHeatmapOverlay.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        ivHeatmapOverlay.visibility = View.VISIBLE
                    }
                } else {
                    ivHeatmapOverlay.visibility = View.GONE
                }
            }
        }
        settingsBody.addView(btnHeatmapToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(30)).apply {
            bottomMargin = dpToPx(3)
        })

        val btnShapeToggle = Button(this).apply {
            text = if (currentIsCircle) "🔘 Форма: Круг" else "🔲 Форма: Прямоугольник"
            textSize = 9f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#161B22"))
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dpToPx(4), 0, dpToPx(4), 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                vibrateFeedback(20L)
                currentIsCircle = !currentIsCircle
                text = if (currentIsCircle) "🔘 Форма: Круг" else "🔲 Форма: Прямоугольник"
                triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef()
            }
        }
        settingsBody.addView(btnShapeToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(30)))

        val btnCancelAction = Button(this).apply {
            text = "ОТМЕНИТЬ КАЛИБРОВКУ"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 10f
            setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
            backgroundTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.parseColor("#F04438"))
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, 0)
            setOnClickListener {
                pendingRecomputeRunnable?.let { recomputeHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
                currentTrimmedMask?.let {
                    if (it != rawTemplateBitmap) {
                        it.recycle()
                    }
                }
                if (!screenshot.isRecycled) {
                    screenshot.recycle()
                }
                if (existingConfig == null && !rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled) {
                    rawTemplateBitmap.recycle()
                }
                safeRemoveView(overlayView)
                restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                if (existingConfig != null) showEditDialog(existingConfig)
            }
        }
        settingsBody.addView(btnCancelAction, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(32)).apply {
            topMargin = dpToPx(4)
        })

        settingsCard.addView(settingsBody)

        btnCollapse.setOnClickListener {
            vibrateFeedback(20L)
            isMenuCollapsed = !isMenuCollapsed
            settingsBody.visibility = if (isMenuCollapsed) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
            btnCollapse.text = if (isMenuCollapsed) "▲ Развернуть" else "▼ Свернуть"
        }

        val primaryTargetY = if (initialCandidates.isNotEmpty()) initialCandidates.first().rect.top else (screenH / 2)
        val initialCardY = if (primaryTargetY > (screenH * 0.55f)) {
            dpToPx(75).toFloat()
        } else {
            (screenH - dpToPx(320)).toFloat().coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(80).toFloat())
        }

        val initialCardX = ((screenW - cardWidthPx) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        settingsCard.translationX = initialCardX
        settingsCard.translationY = initialCardY

        topHeaderRow.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initX = 0f; private var initY = 0f
            private var touchX = 0f; private var touchY = 0f
            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initX = settingsCard.translationX
                        initY = settingsCard.translationY
                        touchX = event.rawX
                        touchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val newX = (initX + (event.rawX - touchX)).coerceIn(0f, (screenW - cardWidthPx).toFloat())
                        val newY = (initY + (event.rawY - touchY)).coerceIn(dpToPx(20).toFloat(), (screenH - dpToPx(50)).toFloat())
                        settingsCard.translationX = newX
                        settingsCard.translationY = newY
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        var renderCandidatesRef: () -> Unit = {}

        renderCandidatesRef = {
            candidatesContainer.removeAllViews()
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] СТАРТ ОТРИСОВКИ. currentCandidates: ${currentCandidates.size}, rawTemplateBitmap: ${rawTemplateBitmap.width}x${rawTemplateBitmap.height}, isRecycled: ${rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled}")

            if (!screenshot.isRecycled && !rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled) {
                val trimmedMaskBitmap = currentTrimmedMask ?: run {
                    logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] Кэш маски пуст. Генерация маски...")
                    val currentMask = generateSmartMask(rawTemplateBitmap, currentIsCircle, currentBgTolerance)
                    val refined = refineAndTrimSmartMask(currentMask, 0, 0)
                    logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] Сгенерирована маска: ${refined.trimmedBitmap.width}x${refined.trimmedBitmap.height}")
                    refined.trimmedBitmap.also { currentTrimmedMask = it }
                }

                logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] trimmedMaskBitmap: ${trimmedMaskBitmap.width}x${trimmedMaskBitmap.height}, isRecycled: ${trimmedMaskBitmap.isRecycled}")

                val displayList = if (currentCandidates.isNotEmpty()) currentCandidates else {
                    val defaultW = rawTemplateBitmap.width.coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(36))
                    val defaultH = rawTemplateBitmap.height.coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(36))
                    val centerX = (screenW - defaultW) / 2
                    val centerY = (screenH - defaultH) / 2
                    logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] Список кандидатов пуст! Добавление FALLBACK центрального элемента.")
                    listOf(MatchCandidate(Point(centerX + defaultW / 2, centerY + defaultH / 2), Rect(centerX, centerY, centerX + defaultW, centerY + defaultH), 1.0f))
                }

                for ((idx, cand) in displayList.withIndex()) {
                    val pct = (cand.score * 100).toInt()
                    val colorHex = when {
                        pct >= currentSimilarity -> "#00F5D4"
                        pct >= (currentSimilarity - 12) -> "#FFB703"
                        else -> "#F04438"
                    }

                    val candW = cand.rect.width().coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(36))
                    val candH = cand.rect.height().coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(36))
                    logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "[renderCandidatesRef] Добавление View кандидата #$idx: rect: ${cand.rect}, score: ${cand.score}, candW: $candW, candH: $candH")

                    val candView = FrameLayout(this@MyAutoClickService).apply {
                        clipChildren = false
                        clipToPadding = false

                        val beaconHalo = View(context).apply {
                            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                                shape = if (currentIsCircle) android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable.OVAL else android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable.RECTANGLE
                                if (!currentIsCircle) cornerRadius = dpToPx(6).toFloat()
                                setColor(Color.parseColor(if (pct >= currentSimilarity) "#4000F5D4" else "#30FFB703"))
                                setStroke(dpToPx(3f), Color.parseColor(colorHex))
                            }
                        }
                        addView(beaconHalo, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

                        val centerReticle = TextView(context).apply {
                            text = "⌖"
                            textSize = 20f
                            setTextColor(Color.parseColor(colorHex))
                            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
                        }
                        addView(centerReticle, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

                        val previewView = ImageView(context).apply {
                            setImageBitmap(trimmedMaskBitmap)
                            scaleType = ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_CENTER
                            alpha = 0.95f
                        }
                        val pad = dpToPx(2)
                        addView(previewView, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT).apply {
                            setMargins(pad, pad, pad, pad)
                        })

                        setOnClickListener {
                            vibrateFeedback(35L)
                            safeRemoveView(overlayView)
                            pendingRecomputeRunnable?.let { recomputeHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }

                            bgScannerExecutor.execute {
                                try {
                                    val ts = System.currentTimeMillis()
                                    val targetDir = File(filesDir, "templates/default").apply { mkdirs() }
                                    val tPath = existingTemplatePath ?: File(targetDir, "mask_$ts.png").absolutePath
                                    val fPath = existingTemplatePath?.replace("mask_", "full_") ?: File(targetDir, "full_$ts.png").absolutePath

                                    saveBitmapToPng(trimmedMaskBitmap, File(tPath))
                                    if (existingConfig == null && !screenshot.isRecycled) {
                                        saveBitmapToPng(screenshot, File(fPath))
                                    }

                                    val metaFile = getTemplateMetadataFile(tPath)
                                    val meta = if (metaFile.exists()) JSONObject(metaFile.readText()) else JSONObject()
                                    meta.put("similarityPercent", currentSimilarity)
                                    meta.put("bgTolerance", currentBgTolerance)
                                    meta.put("isContourMode", currentIsContourMode)
                                    meta.put("isAnimMode", currentIsAnimMode)
                                    meta.put("isSubpixelMode", existingConfig?.isSubpixelMode ?: false)
                                    meta.put("originX", cand.rect.left)
                                    meta.put("originY", cand.rect.top)
                                    meta.put("originW", cand.rect.width())
                                    meta.put("originH", cand.rect.height())
                                    meta.put("isCircleShape", currentIsCircle)

                                    val smartData = analyzeTemplate(trimmedMaskBitmap)
                                    for (key in smartData.keys()) {
                                        meta.put(key, smartData.get(key))
                                    }
                                    FileOutputStream(metaFile).use { out -> out.write(meta.toString().toByteArray()) }

                                    uiExecutor.execute {
                                        loadAllTemplatesFromDisk()
                                        val newIndex = globalTemplatesNames.indexOf(tPath)

                                        if (existingConfig == null) {
                                            addNewActionAtPosition(cand.point.x.toFloat(), cand.point.y.toFloat(), 1000L, ActionType.TRIGGER, newIndex)
                                            val newAction = actionsList.last()
                                            newAction.similarityPercent = currentSimilarity
                                            newAction.isContourMode = currentIsContourMode
                                            newAction.isAnimMode = currentIsAnimMode
                                            newAction.isSubpixelMode = currentIsSubpixelMode

                                            if (newIndex != -1 && !newAction.multiTemplateIndices.contains(newIndex)) {
                                                newAction.multiTemplateIndices.add(newIndex)
                                            }

                                            Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "✅ Шаблон сохранен и применен!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                                        } else {
                                            existingConfig.selectedTemplateIndex = newIndex
                                            existingConfig.similarityPercent = currentSimilarity
                                            existingConfig.isContourMode = currentIsContourMode
                                            existingConfig.isAnimMode = currentIsAnimMode
                                            existingConfig.isSubpixelMode = existingConfig.isSubpixelMode
                                            if (newIndex != -1 && !existingConfig.multiTemplateIndices.contains(newIndex)) {
                                                existingConfig.multiTemplateIndices.add(newIndex)
                                            }
                                            Toast.makeText(this@MyAutoClickService, "✅ Калибровка завершена!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                                            showEditDialog(existingConfig)
                                        }
                                    }
                                } catch (e: Exception) {
                                    logError(this@MyAutoClickService, e)
                                    restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                                } finally {
                                    uiExecutor.execute {
                                        currentTrimmedMask?.let {
                                            if (it != rawTemplateBitmap) {
                                                it.recycle()
                                            }
                                        }
                                        if (!screenshot.isRecycled) {
                                            screenshot.recycle()
                                        }
                                        if (existingConfig == null && !rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled) {
                                            rawTemplateBitmap.recycle()
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    val candParams = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(candW, candH).apply {
                        gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
                        leftMargin = cand.rect.left
                        topMargin = cand.rect.top
                    }
                    candidatesContainer.addView(candView, candParams)

                    val tvScoreBadge = TextView(this@MyAutoClickService).apply {
                        text = "🎯 #${idx + 1} ($pct%)"
                        setTextColor(Color.parseColor(colorHex))
                        textSize = 10f
                        setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD)
                        val padX = dpToPx(6); val padY = dpToPx(2)
                        setPadding(padX, padY, padX, padY)
                        background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                            setColor(Color.parseColor("#EE0D1117"))
                            cornerRadius = dpToPx(6).toFloat()
                            setStroke(dpToPx(1.2f), Color.parseColor(colorHex))
                        }
                        gravity = Gravity.CENTER
                    }

                    val badgeParams = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT).apply {
                        gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
                        leftMargin = (cand.rect.left + (cand.rect.width() / 2) - dpToPx(26)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(8))
                        topMargin = (cand.rect.top - dpToPx(24)).coerceAtLeast(dpToPx(35))
                    }
                    candidatesContainer.addView(tvScoreBadge, badgeParams)
                }
            }
        }

        triggerRealtimeRecomputeRef = {
            pendingRecomputeRunnable?.let { recomputeHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
            val task = Runnable {
                bgScannerExecutor.execute {
                    if (!screenshot.isRecycled && !rawTemplateBitmap.isRecycled) {
                        val dynamicMask = generateSmartMask(rawTemplateBitmap, currentIsCircle, currentBgTolerance)
                        val refined = refineAndTrimSmartMask(dynamicMask, 0, 0)
                        val newTrimmedMask = refined.trimmedBitmap

                        val metaObj = JSONObject().apply {
                            put("similarityPercent", currentSimilarity)
                            put("isContourMode", currentIsContourMode)
                            put("isAnimMode", currentIsAnimMode)
                            put("isSubpixelMode", currentIsSubpixelMode)
                        }

                        val dynamicCandidates = findTemplateCandidatesCoarseFine(
                            screenshot,
                            newTrimmedMask,
                            metaObj,
                            existingTemplatePath ?: "",
                            minSimilarityPercent = 45
                        )

                        uiExecutor.execute {
                            updateTrimmedMask(newTrimmedMask)
                            currentCandidates = dynamicCandidates.toMutableList()
                            renderCandidatesRef()
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            pendingRecomputeRunnable = task
            recomputeHandler.postDelayed(task, 100L)
        }

        renderCandidatesRef()

        val settingsParams = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(cardWidthPx, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
        }
        overlayView.addView(settingsCard, settingsParams)

        val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            getOverlayType(), WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES
            }
        }

        safeAddView(overlayView, params)
    }

    private fun startTemplateCalibration(config: ActionConfig) {
        if (config.selectedTemplateIndex !in globalTemplates.indices) {
            logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "Ошибка: selectedTemplateIndex (${config.selectedTemplateIndex}) вне диапазона globalTemplates (${globalTemplates.size})")
            return
        }
        isCalibrationCancelled = false
        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "Запуск калибровки для шаблона #${config.selectedTemplateIndex + 1}")

        hideControlPanelTemporarily(30000L)
        setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(false)

        val laserContainer = FrameLayout(this).apply {
            clipChildren = false
            clipToPadding = false
        }
        val laserLine = View(this).apply {
            background = android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(Color.parseColor("#00F5D4"))
            }
            elevation = dpToPx(12).toFloat()
        }
        laserContainer.addView(laserLine, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dpToPx(4)).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
            topMargin = 0
        })

        val hudParams = WindowManager.LayoutParams(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            getOverlayType(),
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            PixelFormat.TRANSLUCENT
        ).apply {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                layoutInDisplayCutoutMode = WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES
            }
        }

        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                takeScreenshot(
                    Display.DEFAULT_DISPLAY,
                    bgScannerExecutor,
                    object : TakeScreenshotCallback {
                        override fun onSuccess(screenshotResult: ScreenshotResult) {
                            setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                            val hwBuffer = screenshotResult.hardwareBuffer
                            try {
                                logAppEvent(this@MyAutoClickService, "CALIBRATION", "Скриншот успешно захвачен. Запуск HUD...")
                                val hwBitmap = Bitmap.wrapHardwareBuffer(hwBuffer, screenshotResult.colorSpace)
                                val softwareBitmap = hwBitmap?.copy(Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888, false)
                                if (softwareBitmap != null) {
                                    uiExecutor.execute {
                                        safeAddView(laserContainer, hudParams)
                                        laserLine.animate()
                                            .translationY(resources.displayMetrics.heightPixels.toFloat())
                                            .setDuration(420)
                                            .setInterpolator(android.view.animation.AccelerateDecelerateInterpolator())
                                            .withEndAction {
                                                safeRemoveView(laserContainer)
                                                bgScannerExecutor.execute {
                                                    runSmartCalibration(softwareBitmap, config)
                                                }
                                            }
                                            .start()
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    logAppEvent(this@MyAutoClickService, "ERROR", "softwareBitmap == null")
                                    uiExecutor.execute { restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                                }
                            } catch (e: Exception) {
                                logError(this@MyAutoClickService, e)
                                uiExecutor.execute { restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                            } finally {
                                hwBuffer.close()
                            }
                        }

                        override fun onFailure(errorCode: Int) {
                            setOverlaysVisibleForCapture(true)
                            logAppEvent(this@MyAutoClickService, "ERROR", "takeScreenshot onFailure. Код: $errorCode")
                            uiExecutor.execute { restoreControlPanelImmediately() }
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
        }, 200L)
    }

    private fun runSmartCalibration(
        bitmap: Bitmap,
        config: ActionConfig
    ) {
        val template = globalTemplates[config.selectedTemplateIndex]
        val templatePath = globalTemplatesNames[config.selectedTemplateIndex]
        val meta = loadTemplateMetadata(templatePath)
        val isCircle = meta?.optBoolean("isCircleShape", true) ?: true

        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "Поиск совпадений на экране с базовым порогом 45%...")

        val candidates = findTemplateCandidatesCoarseFine(
            bitmap,
            template,
            meta,
            templatePath,
            minSimilarityPercent = 45,
            isCancelled = { isCalibrationCancelled }
        )

        logAppEvent(this, "CALIBRATION", "Найдено кандидатов: ${candidates.size}")

        uiExecutor.execute {
            if (isCalibrationCancelled) {
                restoreControlPanelImmediately()
                showEditDialog(config)
                bitmap.recycle()
                return@execute
            }
            showCandidatesSelectionOverlay(
                bitmap,
                template,
                candidates,
                isCircle,
                config,
                templatePath
            )
        }
    }
}

--- START OF FILE: app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_add.xml ---

--- START OF FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/autotap/MatchingEngineTest.kt ---
package com.example.autotap

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class MatchingEngineTest {

    @Test
    fun testIsotropicScalingCalculation() {
        val srcW = 1080
        val srcH = 2400
        val curW = 1920
        val curH = 1080

        val sX = curW.toFloat() / srcW.toFloat()
        val sY = curH.toFloat() / srcH.toFloat()
        val uniformScale = Math.min(sX, sY)

        assertTrue("Uniform scale must be positive", uniformScale > 0f)
        assertTrue("Uniform scale must prevent stretching", uniformScale <= sX && uniformScale <= sY)
    }

    @Test
    fun testOrientationGuard() {
        var srcW = 1080
        var srcH = 2400
        val srcIsLandscape = false
        val curIsLandscape = true

        if (srcIsLandscape != curIsLandscape) {
            val temp = srcW
            srcW = srcH
            srcH = temp
        }

        assertEquals(2400, srcW)
        assertEquals(1080, srcH)
    }
}

--- END OF FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/autotap/MatchingEngineTest.kt ---
